### [99] [$(5+ε)$-Approximation of Fréchet Distance in Strongly Subquadratic Time](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06864)
*Lenny Liu,Jihan Wang*

Main category: cs.CG

TL;DR: 本文针对连续和离散Fréchet距离问题，提出了随机化的(5+ε)-近似算法，在运行时间上分别达到Õ_{d,ε}(n m^{8/9})和Õ_{d,ε}(n m^{4/5})，优于此前Cheng等人(7+ε)-近似的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 改进现有Fréchet距离近似算法的近似比和运行时间，特别是超越Cheng等人(2025)的(7+ε)-近似结果。

Method: 通过辅助替代曲线直接认证边界到边界的可达性，避免转换回输入子曲线从而减少一次三角不等式损失；采用两尺度宏替代搜索结合二元辅助转移结构，离散情形利用离散自由空间图中的精确平面可达性获得更快界限。

Result: 连续Fréchet距离算法运行时间Õ_{d,ε}(n m^{8/9})，离散Fréchet距离算法运行时间Õ_{d,ε}(n m^{4/5})，近似比均为(5+ε)。

Conclusion: 通过改进可达性认证策略和搜索结构，在近似比和运行时间上均取得了优于现有工作的结果。

Abstract: We give randomized $(5+ε)$-approximation algorithms for both the continuous and discrete Fréchet distances on arbitrary two polygonal curves $τ$ and $σ$ in $\mathbb R^d$ for fixed $d$, with $n$ and $m\le n$ vertices respectively. Our algorithm for continuous Fréchet runs in $\widetilde O_{d,ε}(n m^{8/9})$ time, and our algorithm for discrete Fréchet runs in $\widetilde O_{d,ε}(n m^{4/5})$ time. These bounds improve the recent strongly subquadratic constant-factor approximation algorithms of Cheng, Huang, and Zhang~\cite{cheng2025constant}, which give $(7+ε)$-approximations.
  The approximation improvement comes from certifying long boundary-to-boundary reachability directly through auxiliary surrogate curves, avoiding an extra conversion back to input subcurves and hence removing one triangle-inequality loss. The running-time improvement comes from a two-scale macro-surrogate search combined with dyadic auxiliary-transfer structures, with the discrete case gaining a faster bound from exact planar reachability in the discrete free-space graph.

</details>


### [100] [Benchmarking and Engineering Data Structures for Spherical Range Queries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07367)
*Thomas Bläsius,Jean-Pierre von der Heydt,Tobias Kempf,Dennis Kobert,Nikolai Maas*

Main category: cs.CG

TL;DR: 本文全面评估了现有空间索引结构在球形范围查询上的性能，并提出了SPRK-tree（排序投影半径KD树），该变体通过激进子树剪枝、排序投影叶节点和实现优化，在几乎全部基准测试中取得最快查询时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有空间数据结构在回答球形范围查询时，理论最坏情况界过于悲观，现有平均情况分析受限且难以比较，导致为特定应用选择最优结构困难。

Method: 首先对多种最先进空间索引在多样化基准（包含图嵌入和多个真实世界数据集，规模达1000万点，维度2-960）上进行全面评估；然后提出SPRK-tree，结合激进子树剪枝（半径缩减）、排序投影叶节点和精细实现优化。

Result: SPRK-tree在几乎所有基准测试中取得最快查询时间，仅在少数场景中排名第二。

Conclusion: 通过全面基准评估和SPRK-tree的提出，有效解决了球形范围查询中空间索引选择困难的问题，SPRK-tree成为高性能的通用解决方案。

Abstract: Spherical range queries are a fundamental primitive for working with spatial data. Many spatial data structures have been developed to answer these queries, but choosing the optimal one for a specific application is a difficult task. This is because theoretical worst-case bounds are often overly pessimistic, and existing average-case analyses are rather restricted and hard to compare.
  We address this problem with two main contributions. First, we present a comprehensive evaluation of state-of-the-art spatial indices across a diverse set of benchmarks. This includes a new benchmark based on graph embeddings alongside multiple real-world datasets from the literature. Our benchmark covers instances scaling up to 10M points and ranging between 2 and 960 dimensions.
  Second, we introduce the Sorted-Projection Radius KD-tree (SPRK-tree), a high-performance KD-tree variant. The SPRK-tree combines aggressive subtree pruning via radius reduction, sorted projection-based leaf nodes, and careful implementation optimizations. It consistently achieves the fastest query times in almost all benchmarks, and ranks second in the few remaining cases.

</details>


<div id='math.NA'></div>

# math.NA [[Back]](#toc)

### [101] [Adaptive, Matrix-Free Low-Rank Approximation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06758)
*Arnel I. Smith,Elly Do,Chao Chen*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一系列自适应的、矩阵无关的随机化QB算法，用于固定容差的低秩近似。这些方法仅通过矩阵-向量乘积访问算子，利用随机化误差指示器在Frobenius范数或谱范数下估计残差范数到机器精度，并通过矩阵无关的秩剪枝步骤解耦计算块大小与最终秩，从而在不过度估计秩的情况下使用大规模BLAS-3友好的块。实验表明，在面对不同奇异值衰减的测试矩阵时，所提方法能够以高概率达到接近截断SVD最优的秩，同时满足预设容差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在矩阵无关（matrix-free）场景下，矩阵或线性算子只能通过矩阵-向量乘积访问，且其秩需要自适应地确定以满足给定的误差容限。现有方法在计算块大小与最终秩之间往往存在耦合，导致要么块大小受限，要么秩被过度估计。

Method: 提出一类自适应的、矩阵无关的随机化QB算法。核心组件包括：(1) 随机化误差指示器，通过随机草图直接估计残差的Frobenius范数或谱范数，精度可达机器精度；(2) 矩阵无关的秩剪枝步骤，将计算块大小与最终秩解耦，允许使用大的、BLAS-3友好的块而不至于过度估计秩；(3) 无伴随变体，仅使用前向算子即可返回正交基。

Result: 在具有不同奇异值衰减的测试矩阵上，所提方法能够以高概率达到接近截断SVD最优的秩，同时满足预设的误差容限。

Conclusion: 本文提出的自适应矩阵无关随机化QB算法有效解决了矩阵无关场景下的固定容差低秩近似问题，在保证精度和概率可靠性的同时，实现了计算效率与秩估计最优性的良好平衡。

Abstract: We study fixed-tolerance low-rank approximation in the matrix-free setting, where a matrix or linear operator $\mathbf{A}$ is accessible only through matrix-vector products and its rank must be determined adaptively to meet a prescribed error tolerance. We introduce a family of adaptive, matrix-free randomized QB algorithms. A randomized error indicator estimates the residual norm -- in either the Frobenius or the spectral norm -- directly from a random sketch, remaining accurate down to machine precision. A matrix-free rank-pruning step decouples the computational block size from the final rank, so that large, BLAS-3-friendly blocks can be used without over-estimating the rank, and an adjoint-free variant returns the orthonormal basis using only the forward operator. Across test matrices with diverse singular-value decays, the proposed methods attain ranks close to the truncated-SVD optimum while meeting the prescribed tolerance with high probability.

</details>


### [102] [A locking free mixed FEM based on a pure pseudostress based formulation for the elasticity eigenproblem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06890)
*Arbaz Khan,Felipe Lepe,Jesus Vellojin*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文针对弹性特征值问题，提出了一种无锁定的混合有限元公式，仅用伪应力张量表示，无需强制对称性，并通过后处理恢复位移。采用张量化的标准有限元族离散，证明了收敛性和先验误差界，同时进行了后验误差分析，数值实验验证了理论结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决弹性特征值问题中传统公式需要强制对称性约束以及锁定现象的问题，提出一种更简洁、无锁定的混合公式。

Method: 基于仅含伪应力张量的混合公式，采用张量化标准有限元族离散 $\boldsymbol{\mathcal{H}}(\bdiv)$ 空间，位移通过后处理恢复；利用非紧算子理论证明收敛性和先验误差界，并进行后验误差分析证明指示子的可靠性和有效性。

Result: 证明了该方法的收敛性和先验误差估计，后验误差指示子具有可靠性和有效性，数值实验在不同几何和物理配置下验证了理论结果。

Conclusion: 所提出的无对称强制约束的混合有限元方法能够有效求解弹性特征值问题，避免了锁定现象，理论和数值结果一致支持该方法的正确性和实用性。

Abstract: We analyze a novel locking-free mixed formulation for the elasticity eigenvalue problem in both two and three dimensions, expressed exclusively in terms of the pseudostress tensor. An important feature of this formulation is that it does not require the enforcement of symmetry, either in a weak or strong sense. The displacement of the structure is recovered via a postprocess of the computed pseudostress. We introduce a mixed finite element method based in the tensorial version of the standard families of finite elements to discretize the space $\boldsymbol{\mathcal{H}}(\bdiv)$. We prove convergence and a priori error estimates under the theory of non-compact operators. Additionally, we perform an a posteriori error analysis for the problem, proving reliability and efficiency of the proposed indicator. We validate our theoretical results with numerical tests on different geometrical and physical configurations.

</details>


### [103] [RPLSS: A randomized projected linear systems solver](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06917)
*Meng-Long Xiao,Tao Li,Deanna Needell*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出统一随机化投影线性系统求解器（RPLSS）框架，通过随机行/列选择策略仅需部分矩阵信息，解决了传统PLSS需访问全矩阵的计算瓶颈，适用于欠定/超定、一致/非一致线性系统，并证明其具有有限终止性和指数收敛速率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统PLSS每次迭代需访问整个系数矩阵，计算代价高昂，难以应用于超大规模问题或含缺失数据的场景。为此，作者希望设计一种仅需部分矩阵信息的随机化求解框架，以降低计算开销。

Method: 提出统一RPLSS框架，包括：(1) 随机化高斯Kaczmarz方法及其扩展作为行求解器；(2) 随机化坐标下降方法作为列求解器。核心策略是每步迭代仅通过行或列的随机采样获取部分矩阵信息，替代全矩阵访问。

Result: 理论证明所提方法继承了PLSS的有限终止性，同时达到指数收敛速率，克服了传统随机Kaczmarz和坐标下降方法收敛缓慢的问题。数值实验表明，尤其在大量数据缺失场景下，方法优于现有最先进随机化方法。

Conclusion: 提出RPLSS框架有效缓解了PLSS在全矩阵访问上的计算瓶颈，同时保持了有限终止性和更快的收敛速率，为大规模及缺失数据场景下的线性系统求解提供了高效方案。

Abstract: The projected linear system solver (PLSS), by incrementally appending columns to a random or deterministic sketching matrix, provides an attractive finite termination property for consistent linear systems. Nevertheless, a critical computational bottleneck of PLSS is accessing the whole coefficient matrix per iteration, making it prohibitive for extremely large-scale problems or applications with missing data. To alleviate this limitation, we propose a unified randomized PLSS (RPLSS) framework, built upon the tailored randomized row or column selection strategies that require only partial matrix information per iteration, for solving a general linear system, whether it is under- or overdetermined, and whether it is consistent or not. Within this framework, we develop a randomized Gaussian Kaczmarz method and its extended variant as row-action solvers, and randomized coordinate descent variants as column-action solvers. Theoretically, we prove that our methods inherit the finite termination property of PLSS, while achieving an exponential convergence rate, overcoming the sluggish convergence inherent in conventional randomized Kaczmarz and coordinate descent methods. Numerical experiments demonstrate the superiority of our method against state-of-the-art randomized methods, particularly in scenarios with large missing data.

</details>


### [104] [Solution Analysis of Tensor Equation $\mathcal{A} \ltimes \mathcal{X} \ltimes \mathcal{B}= \mathcal{C}$ via Semi Tensor Product with t-product](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07006)
*Bhawna Garg,Ranjan Kumar Das*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文研究了基于半张量积与t-积的张量方程$\mathcal{A\ltimes X\ltimes B=C}$，给出了解存在的充要条件，刻画了解的显式结构（Toeplitz和Circulant），并通过实例验证了结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 张量方程在信号处理、图像重建等领域有广泛应用，现有研究多集中于经典张量积下的方程，而半张量积与t-积框架下的张量方程解的存在性与结构特性尚未被充分刻画。

Method: 针对未知向量$\mathcal{X}$，建立了解存在的充要条件；针对矩阵值和高阶张量值未知$\mathcal{X}$，通过相容性要求判定可解性；进一步刻画了$\mathcal{C}$的Toeplitz和Circulant显式结构。

Result: 给出了不同张量类型下解的存在性判定准则，并揭示了结果张量$\mathcal{C}$的Toeplitz与Circulant结构特征。

Conclusion: 本文在半张量积与t-积框架下系统解决了张量方程的可解性与结构表征问题，为相关应用提供了理论支撑。

Abstract: This paper focuses on the analysis of the tensor equation $\mathcal{A\ltimes X\ltimes B=C}$, formulated via the semi tensor product with t-product. For the unknown vector $\mathcal{X}$, we establish a necessary and sufficient condition that provides an equivalence criterion for the existence of solutions. For matrix valued and higher-order tensor valued unknown $\mathcal{X}$, solvability is determined by corresponding compatibility requirements. Moreover, the explicit structure (Toeplitz and Circulant) of $\mathcal{C}$ is characterized. The derived results are supported by several illustrative examples.

</details>


### [105] [Numerical solutions of an accurate diffuse interface model of the incompressible resistive MHD free surface flow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07025)
*Maojun Li,Jiancheng Wang,Zeyu Xia,Liwei Xu*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种热力学一致的扩散界面方法模拟不可压缩电阻性磁流体（MHD）自由表面流动，建立了向尖锐界面模型的渐近收敛性，设计了满足磁场散度自由约束的全解耦线性有限元方案，并通过气泡动力学中的磁阻尼效应验证了模型的可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MHD自由表面流动模拟中，界面追踪方法复杂且计算成本高，缺乏兼顾热力学一致性、散度约束保持和高效求解的数值框架。

Method: 采用热力学一致的扩散界面方法描述运动界面；通过匹配渐近分析证明模型向尖锐界面模型的收敛性；设计全解耦线性有限元格式，在离散层面保持磁场散度自由约束。

Result: 数值验证表明模型能有效捕捉磁阻尼效应对气泡动力学的影响；定量比较结果与无感应MHD模型及尖锐界面ALE模型吻合良好。

Conclusion: 所提出的扩散界面MHD模型兼具热力学一致性、理论收敛性及数值鲁棒性，无需复杂界面捕捉方案即可准确模拟自由表面MHD流动。

Abstract: In this paper, we derive a new model to simulate the incompressible resistive magnetohydrodynamic (MHD) free surface flow. A thermodynamically consistent diffuse interface method is adopted to characterize the moving interface in the modeling process. The formal convergence of the proposed MHD free surface flow model to the sharp interface model is established via a matched asymptotic argument, and the model can be solved without the need for sophisticated free surface capturing schemes. We design a fully decoupled linear finite element scheme that preserves the divergence-free constraint of the magnetic field at a discrete level. The reliability and robustness of the proposed model and algorithm are validated through numerical investigations of the magnetic damping effect on bubble dynamics. In particular, we provide a quantitative numerical comparison of the present results with those obtained from an inductionless MHD model and a sharp interface arbitrary Lagrangian--Eulerian model.

</details>


### [106] [Mixed precision explicit numerical methods for ordinary differential equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07080)
*M Al Sayed Ali,S Bernard,A Marzorati,J Rouzaud-Cornabas*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出混合精度显式数值方法求解大规模生物ODE系统，在保持精度的同时实现最高2倍加速，优于纯单精度方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模生物过程ODE模型通常非线性、复杂且高维，显式数值方法虽灵活但稳定区域有限导致计算成本高，需降低计算开销。

Method: 开发多种混合精度显式方法，在数值方法选定部分使用低精度（单精度）运算，结合MPI实现串行与并行版本，以float-double混合精度执行。

Result: 在两个大规模生物基准ODE模型上，混合方法相比纯双精度实现最高加速2倍且精度一致；减小时间步长能提升混合方法的性能与鲁棒性；纯单精度方法无法收敛。

Conclusion: 部分降低显式方法中算术精度是有效策略，混合精度在保持精度的前提下显著降低大规模ODE求解的计算开销。

Abstract: Our objective is to solve large systems of ordinary differential equations (ODEs) commonly used to model biological processes. These equations are typically nonlinear, complex, and high-dimensional. In computational biology, such ODEs are generally solved using numerical methods. In this work, we focus on explicit numerical methods because of their flexibility. However, their limited stability regions may result in high computational costs. To mitigate this issue, we investigate mixed precision algorithms designed to reduce computational effort by performing selected parts of the numerical method in lower arithmetic precision. We develop several mixed precision explicit methods and assess their performance on two large scale biological benchmark ODE models. Our theoretical analysis highlights the effectiveness of partially reducing arithmetic precision within explicit methods. Numerical experiments demonstrate that our mixed methods-implemented in both sequential and parallel versions using MPI-combining single (float) and double precision arithmetic can achieve up to twice the speed of a fully double precision implementation while preserving the same level of accuracy. Furthermore, the results indicate that decreasing the timestep improves the performance and robustness of our mixed methods, while the single precision method fails to converge.

</details>


### [107] [A framework for generating nonconforming triangular meshes with multiple discretization layers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07242)
*I. L. Semenov,M. M. Becker*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于前沿Delaunay算法生成非一致三角形网格的框架，通过将体区域中的结构化网格分组聚合为复合单元，实现多层离散化网格的构建，支持自适应网格加密并易于集成到现有求解器中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的非一致网格生成方法在多层离散化方面存在生成复杂或难以与现有求解器集成的问题，需要一种能够利用网格固有结构特性高效生成多层非一致三角形网格的框架。

Method: 利用前沿Delaunay算法生成均匀尺寸网格的体区域中呈现的规则三角形晶格结构，通过将体区域中的单元分组聚合为更大的复合单元，反复应用该过程实现多层离散化层的生成；对于复杂几何，进一步在每个子域内生成多层离散化层以创建复合多域网格。

Result: 展示了所提框架生成的网格示例，并讨论了用于网格细化和粗化的后处理策略，验证了生成网格适用于自适应网格加密技术。

Conclusion: 该框架能够高效生成多层非一致三角形网格，且可以与现有网格生成器和有限元求解器无缝集成。

Abstract: We present a framework for generating nonconforming triangular meshes with multiple discretization layers. The framework exploits characteristic structural properties of meshes produced by a frontal Delaunay algorithm with uniform element size. The bulk region of such meshes exhibits a structured pattern resembling a regular triangular lattice. Owing to this structure, the bulk region can be coarsened by grouping elements into connected subsets of larger composite elements. When applied repeatedly, this procedure produces a mesh with multiple discretization layers. For complex geometries, the framework can be used to create composite multidomain meshes, where multiple discretization layers are generated within each subdomain. We present examples of meshes generated by the proposed framework and discuss postprocessing strategies for their refinement and coarsening. The resulting meshes are well suited for the application of adaptive mesh refinement techniques. The proposed framework can be readily integrated with existing mesh generators and finite-element solvers that support nonconforming triangular meshes.

</details>


### [108] [Iterative finite element approximation of non-monotone semilinear diffusion-reaction equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07327)
*Florian Spicher,Thomas P. Wihler*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文研究了针对半线性椭圆边值问题（非线性反应项具有渐近线性增长）的迭代线性化有限元方法。该方法突破了经典理论中非线性项需为单调的限制，采用稳定化IMEX迭代结合一阶有限元离散化，并在分级网格上处理多边形域角点奇异性。通过在角加权Sobolev空间中建立正则性估计，对迭代过程产生的离散Galerkin极限的逼近性质进行了非标准分析，获得了最优先验收敛估计。数值实验验证了理论结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有理论主要处理单调非线性项，而对非单调非线性项（如渐近线性增长的反应项）的迭代线性化有限元方法缺乏系统性分析。多边形域中的角点奇异性进一步增加了数值逼近的难度，需要建立新的收敛性理论框架。

Method: 采用稳定化显式-隐式（IMEX）迭代格式，结合一阶有限元离散在分级网格上逼近；在角加权Sobolev空间中建立正则性估计；对迭代过程的离散Galerkin极限进行非标准逼近性质分析，推导最优先验误差估计。

Result: 获得了离散Galerkin极限的最优先验收敛估计，数值实验验证了理论分析的正确性。

Conclusion: 所提出的迭代线性化有限元方法能够有效处理非单调非线性半线性椭圆问题，并能在具有角点奇异性的多边形域上保持最优收敛率，拓展了经典理论的适用范围。

Abstract: We study iterative linearized finite element methods for the numerical approximation of semilinear elliptic boundary value problems with nonlinear reaction terms of asymptotically linear growth. Our approach reaches considerably beyond the classical theory by allowing for nonlinearities that are not necessarily monotone. We investigate a stabilized implicit-explicit (IMEX) iteration combined with first-order finite element discretizations on graded meshes that are able to resolve corner singularities in polygonal domains. To account for the reduced regularity of the analytical solutions, we establish regularity estimates in corner-weighted Sobolev spaces. The principal novelty of the paper is a non-standard analysis of the approximation properties of the discrete Galerkin limits resulting from the iterative process, which yields optimal a priori convergence estimates. Numerical experiments underline the theoretical findings.

</details>


### [109] [JAX-FVM: A differentiable, entropy-stable finite volume solver on unstructured meshes for compressible flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07385)
*Guillaume de Romémont*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文介绍JAX-FVM，一个基于JAX的完全可微二维非结构化三角网格有限体积法求解器，用于可压缩欧拉和Navier-Stokes方程，支持端到端自动微分、JIT编译及CPU/GPU透明运行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可微CFD框架几乎仅限于结构化网格或谱离散方法，缺乏非结构化网格上的可微有限体积法实现，JAX-FVM填补了这一空白。

Method: 基于JAX实现完整的有限体积法流程：熵守恒Tadmor/Ismail-Roe通量、熵变量Rusanov或Roe耗散、二阶MUSCL重构（最小二乘梯度+Venkatakrishnan限制器）、显式(RK2-4)和矩阵自由隐式(Newton, SDIRK2)时间积分，所有操作均可自动微分。

Result: 完成了标准验证算例的测试，代码开源可用。

Conclusion: JAX-FVM成功将非结构化网格可压缩有限体积法与端到端可微性结合，拓展了可微CFD的适用范围。

Abstract: We present JAX-FVM, an open-source, fully differentiable finite volume method (FVM) for the two-dimensional compressible Euler and Navier-Stokes equations on unstructured triangular meshes. The solver is written entirely in JAX, so that every operation : mesh connectivity, flux evaluation, slope limiting, and time integration is just-in-time compiled, vectorised, and end-to-end differentiable through automatic differentiation (AD), and runs transparently on CPU or GPU. On the numerical side, JAX-FVM is built around an entropy-conservative Tadmor/Ismail-Roe two-point flux supplemented with entropy-variable Rusanov or Roe dissipation, second-order MUSCL reconstruction of primitive variables with least-squares gradients and Venkatakrishnan limiting, and a family of explicit (RK2-4) and matrix-free implicit (Newton, SDIRK2) time integrators whose Jacobian actions are obtained by AD. The combination of an unstructured-mesh compressible FVM with end-to-end differentiability fills a gap left by existing differentiable CFD frameworks, which are almost exclusively restricted to structured grids or spectral discretisations. We describe the governing equations, the discretisation, the software architecture, and a set of standard verification cases. The code is openly available at https://github.com/guigzair/jax_fvm.

</details>


### [110] [Compact Rational Krylov for Parametrized Systems with Application to BEM Frequency Sweeping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07440)
*Kobe Bruyninckx,Daan Huybrechs,Karl Meerbergen*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出将紧凑有理Krylov（CORK）框架应用于参数化线性系统，通过线性化、左右预处理的GMRES方法及CORK加速，高效求解多参数值下的系统解，并应用于Helmholtz散射问题的边界元法频率扫描。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 参数化线性系统P(μ)x=b中矩阵P对参数μ呈非线性依赖，且需对大量参数值求解，传统方法效率低下，亟需一种能一次性为多参数值高效产生近似解的框架。

Method: 首先将参数化系统线性化为大型移位线性系统(A-μB)y=d；然后提出左、右预处理的Krylov子空间GMRES方法求解移位系统；利用CORK框架加速计算；处理参数依赖的右端项b(μ)；通过选择移位控制收敛；允许迭代过程中对移位进行非精确求解。

Result: 该方法能高效地为多参数值同时产生近似解，并成功应用于BEM离散的Helmholtz散射问题频率扫描，利用了稠密但数据稀疏的波数依赖系统矩阵的高效表示。

Conclusion: CORK框架适用于参数化线性系统，结合预处理GMRES可显著提升多参数求解效率，为频域扫描等应用提供有效工具。

Abstract: In parametrized linear systems $\mathsf{P}(μ)\mathsf{x}=\mathsf{b}$ the system matrix $\mathsf{P}$ depends nonlinearly on a parameter $μ$ and solutions are sought for many values of this parameter. We show that the compact rational Krylov (CORK) framework, originally introduced to solve nonlinear eigenvalue problems, can be used to efficiently produce approximate solutions to such a system for many values of the parameter at once. In this approach the parametrized system is first linearized, resulting in a large shifted linear system $(\boldsymbol{\mathsf{A}}-μ\boldsymbol{\mathsf{B}})\boldsymbol{\mathsf{y}}=\boldsymbol{\mathsf{d}}$. We formulate a left- and right-preconditioned rational Krylov GMRES method for shifted linear systems and show how the CORK framework can be used to speed up these methods in the setting of parametrized linear systems. Additionally, we show how to incorporate a right-hand side $\mathsf{b}(μ)$ that also depends on the parameter, how to choose the shifts to steer convergence and how to allow for inexact solves at these shifts throughout the iterations. As an application we consider the 'frequency sweeping' of Helmholtz scattering problems through the Boundary Element Method (BEM), enabled via an efficient representation of the dense but data-sparse wavenumber-dependent system matrix.

</details>


### [111] [An Approximate Bounded Cochain Projection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07457)
*Marc Gerritsma,Suyash Shrestha*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种从无限维Hilbert复形到有限维分片多项式子复形的投影算子构造，在可缩域上满足有界上链投影子的三个性质，在非可缩域上满足近似交换性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的有限元外微积分理论需要一种既能保持交换性又具有一致有界性的投影算子，以更好地处理非可缩域上的问题。

Method: 构造了一个从无限维Hilbert复形（微分k-形式）到有限维分片多项式子复形的投影算子，并分析其在可缩域和非可缩域上的性质。

Result: 在可缩域上，该投影子满足幂等性、Sobolev范数一致有界性以及与外部微分的交换性；在非可缩域上，幂等性和一致有界性仍然成立，交换性可在任意精度下满足。

Conclusion: 该构造为有限元外微积分提供了更通用的工具，尤其对非可缩域上的应用具有重要价值。

Abstract: This paper presents a construction of a projector from an infinite-dimensional Hilbert complex of differential $k$-forms onto a finite-dimensional piecewise polynomial sub-complex. We demonstrate that, on contractable domains, the proposed projector attains the three properties of the Bounded Cochain Projector, namely that the projector is idempotent, uniformly bounded in the Sobolev norm, and it commutes with the exterior derivative. On non-contractible domains, the projector remains idempotent and uniformly bounded, while the commuting property is satisfied up to arbitrary accuracy.

</details>


### [112] [A black-box, multilevel algebraic preconditioning framework for conforming finite elements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07485)
*O. A. Krzysik,B. S. Southworth,G. A. Wimmer*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文阐明了LS-AMG-DD方法中Gram结构存在的条件：在给定自由度覆盖C上，A存在C-局部Gram表示当且仅当A允许C-局部SPSD分裂。将此观点与相容有限元离散联系起来，为LS-AMG-DD应用于有限元问题提供了黑箱路径。数值测试表明该方法在经典AMG需要超过10^5次迭代的问题上具有鲁棒性，且误差比现有AMG方法低2-5个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 澄清LS-AMG-DD方法中Gram结构存在的充要条件，建立其与有限元离散中局部SPSD分裂的自然联系，从而为该方法的通用化应用提供理论基础。

Method: 理论分析：证明A存在C-局部Gram表示当且仅当A存在C-局部SPSD分裂；建立与相容有限元离散的联系；通过数值测试验证方法在经典AMG难以收敛的问题上的性能。

Result: ① 建立了Gram表示与局部SPSD分裂的等价关系；② 发现相容有限元离散自然满足该条件，提供了黑箱式应用路径；③ 在H(div)中的高阶梯散度、H²中各向异性超扩散、向量H¹中线弹性等问题上，经典AMG需10^5+次迭代，而LS-AMG-DD收敛；④ 相同残差容忍度下，LS-AMG-DD误差比其他AMG方法低2-5个数量级。

Conclusion: LS-AMG-DD方法在相容有限元问题中具有通用性和鲁棒性，尤其对经典AMG难以处理的问题表现优异，可作为黑箱预条件器使用。

Abstract: Recently we introduced the least-squares algebraic-multigrid domain-decomposition (LS-AMG-DD) method as a multilevel, algebraic preconditioner for sparse symmetric positive definite (SPD) matrices that admit a Gram representation \(A=G^{\top}G\) \cite{southworth2026lsamgdd}. The factor \(G\) induces a local symmetric positive semidefinite (SPSD) splitting of \(A\) used to define local spectral problems from which an interpolation $P$ is built, and a coarse-level Gram operator induced under Galerkin coarsening, \(A_c=G_c^\top G_c\), for \(G_c:=GP\). This paper clarifies when this Gram structure arises, showing that, on a prescribed degree-of-freedom cover \({\cal C}\), a \({\cal C}\)-local Gram representation of $A$ exists if and only if \(A\) admits a \({\cal C}\)-local SPSD splitting. We then connect this viewpoint to conforming finite-element discretizations, where bilinear forms are naturally assembled from elementwise SPSD energies and therefore admit element-local Gram representations after choosing local factors (e.g., via algebraic factorizations of element blocks). Taken together, these observations provide an essentially black-box route for applying LS-AMG-DD to conforming finite-element problems. Numerical tests illustrate the robustness of the method on several problems for which classical AMG methods require more than $10^5$ iterations to converge, including high-order discretizations of grad--div in \(\hdiv\), anisotropic hyperdiffusion in $H^2$, and linear elasticity in vector \(H^1\). Moreover, in some comparisons with existing AMG methods, LS-AMG-DD produces errors that are 2--5 orders of magnitude smaller, even when all methods are stopped at the same relative residual tolerance.

</details>


### [113] [Explicit stabilized implementation of singly diagonally implicit Runge-Kutta methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07497)
*Ibrahim Almuslimani,Gilles Vilmart,Konstantinos Zygalakis*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的显式稳定化实现方法，用于一类对角隐式龙格-库塔方法，该方法将隐式方法的高阶精度与显式稳定化方法的计算效率相结合，用于求解对流-扩散-反应偏微分方程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 隐式方法虽能避免刚性微分方程的步长限制，但计算成本高；显式稳定化方法在高维扩散问题中效率高但存在稳定性限制。本文旨在融合两种方法的优势，实现兼具高阶精度和低计算成本的数值积分方案。

Method: 将隐式龙格-库塔更新重新表述为修正辅助系统的稳态问题，然后采用受优化技术启发的分区龙格-库塔-切比雪夫方法求解该稳态。

Result: 实现了高阶单对角隐式龙格-库塔方法，其计算成本与标准显式稳定化方法相当，且具有可证明的计算复杂度。

Conclusion: 该方法成功结合了隐式方法和显式稳定化方法的优点，为求解对流-扩散-反应偏微分方程提供了一种高效、高精度的新途径。

Abstract: Implicit methods are a natural approach for the integration of stiff differential equations, to avoid time-step restrictions faced by standard explicit integrators. Explicit stabilised integrators are an alternative to implicit methods, which can be particularly efficient in high-dimensional applications with diffusive terms. Towards the best of both worlds, we introduce a new explicit stabilised implementation of a class of diagonally implicit Runge-Kutta methods. This allows us to implement high-order singly-diagonally-implicit Runge-Kutta methods for advection-diffusion-reaction PDEs with a provable computational cost analogous to that of standard explicit stabilised methods. The main ingredient is to recast the implicit Runge-Kutta update as the steady state of a modified auxiliary system, which is then computed using a partitioned Runge-Kutta-Chebyshev method inspired by optimisation techniques.

</details>


### [114] [Structure-Guided Gauss-Network Method: Linear Advection-Reaction Equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07506)
*Zhiqiang Cai,César Herrera*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 针对最小二乘神经网络（LSNN）方法求解线性对流反应方程时存在的非凸优化难题，本文提出了一种结构引导的高斯-牛顿（SgGN）方法，通过交替更新线性参数（线性求解器）和非线性参数（改进的高斯-牛顿法，显式消除奇异矩阵），在计算成本和精度上均优于Adam优化器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LSNN方法虽能无需先验知识精确逼近不连续解，但其离散化问题为非凸优化，计算强度大且复杂，亟需更高效的优化算法。

Method: 提出SgGN方法，在每次外迭代中：线性参数通过线性求解器计算；非线性参数通过改进的高斯-牛顿法更新，显式移除GN矩阵的奇异性。

Result: 在所有[5]中的测试问题上，SgGN方法在计算成本和精度上均优于常用的Adam优化器。

Conclusion: SgGN方法是一种高效且精确的替代方案，适用于LSNN框架下的非凸优化问题。

Abstract: The least-squares neural network (LSNN) method introduced in [5] for linear advection-reaction equations is capable of accurately approximating discontinuous solutions without a priori knowledge of the interface location. However, the resulting discretization is a non-convex optimization problem that is computationally intensive and complex. In this paper, we propose a structure-guided Gauss-Newton (SgGN) method that alternates between the linear (output) and the nonlinear (hidden layer) parameters. At each outer iteration, the linear parameters are computed by a linear solver, and the nonlinear parameters are updated by a modified Gauss-Newton (GN) method that explicitly removes the singularities of the GN matrix. Numerical experiments for all test problems presented in [5] show that the SgGN method is superior to the Adam optimizer [13], the commonly used first-order optimization algorithm, not only in computational cost but, more importantly, in accuracy

</details>
