### [115] [A spatial duration-augmented framework for drought persistence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06805)
*Touqeer Ahmad,Thordis Linda Thorarinsdottir*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种贝叶斯空间持续时间增强框架，用于干旱持续性建模。研究发现：参考蒸散量公式选择对干旱信号影响微弱，而使用BATs分布可显著提升指数估计（正态性接受率98.4%-99.9%），空间持续时间模型优于非空间模型。结果显示意大利南部（西西里、撒丁岛）具有强持续性和持续时间依赖的恢复风险，北部和阿尔卑斯地区持续时间依赖性近乎为零。干旱表征对分布和持续性假设比对RET公式更敏感。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨异质气候区域的干旱监测需要显式建模干旱持续性，而非仅依赖指数分类。现有方法未能同时捕捉时间记忆和空间依赖性，且未充分评估干旱指数构建中不同公式选择和概率分布的影响。

Method: 提出贝叶斯空间持续时间增强框架，结合时间记忆和空间依赖性；评估三种RET公式（Penman-Monteith、Hargraves、Thornthwaite）和多种概率分布（BATs、GEV等）对标准化气候水平衡的影响；采用0.25°×0.25°高分辨率数据覆盖意大利730个网格。

Result: RET公式选择对干旱信号影响微弱（SPEI序列在各时间尺度上一致）；BATs分布显著优于其他分布（正态性接受率98.4%-99.9%，对比GEV的37%-92%和其他分布的0%-30%）；空间持续时间模型优于非空间模型；意大利南部表现出强持续性和持续时间依赖的恢复风险，北部地区持续时间依赖性近乎为零。

Conclusion: 干旱表征对分布选择和持续性假设比对RET公式选择更敏感；持续时间不敏感的分类会系统性低估意大利南部的长期干旱风险；建议采用BATs分布和空间持续时间模型进行干旱监测。

Abstract: Drought monitoring across heterogeneous climate regions requires explicit modeling of drought persistence rather than relying solely on index classification. We develop a Bayesian spatial, duration-augmented framework for drought persistence that captures both temporal memory and spatial dependence. Because persistence estimates depend on the underlying drought index, we also assess the influence of (i) reference evapotranspiration (RET) formulations and (ii) the choice of probability distribution used to standardize the climatic water balance. The framework is applied to a high-resolution (0.25$^\circ \times$ 0.25$^\circ$) dataset over 730 grid cells across Italy. The choice of RET formulation (Penman-Monteith, Hargraves, Thornthwaite) has only a marginal influence on the overall drought signal: spatially averaged SPEI series at the 3-, 6-, and 12-month scales are identical across methods. By contrast, the proposed bulk-and-tails (BATs) distribution substantially improves index estimation, consistently outperforming commonly used alternative models across all RET methods and timescales and achieving normality acceptance rates of 98.4%--99.9% compared to 37%--92% for the generalized extreme value (GEV) distribution and 0%--30% for others. The spatial duration-augmented model further outperforms a nonspatial counterpart, producing spatially continuous, uncertainty-aware recovery and survival fields. Results reveal a strong south-north gradient in temporal memory: southern peninsular and island regions (Sicily, Sardinia) exhibit pronounced persistence and duration-dependent recovery hazards, whereas northern and Alpine regions show near-zero duration dependence. Drought characterization is more sensitive to the distributional and persistence assumptions than to the RET formulation, and duration-insensitive classifications systematically understate extended drought risk in southern Italy.

</details>


### [116] [Learning Item Embeddings and Hyperparameters for IRT Calibration via Monte Carlo EM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06905)
*James Sharpnack,Kai-Ling Lo*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种预训练方法，利用神经网络生成低维项目嵌入，使生产系统能够在高利害计算机自适应测试（CAT）中使用简单的线性解释性IRT模型进行项目校准，避免实时调参对效度的威胁。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高利害CAT中，新项目因响应数据不足导致参数估计不准确，影响测验分数；神经IRT模型虽强大，但实时调参不切实际且威胁效度。

Method: 提出预启动步骤：训练一个特征网络将项目内容特征映射为低维嵌入$h_j = z(x_j) \in \mathbb{R}^d$，其上使用线性解释性IRT模型（3PL），猜测参数c固定为全局常数；通过MCEM联合拟合特征网络和潜在能力θ。

Result: 在Duolingo英语测试的两个任务类型上，浅层两层ReLU网络（d=6）配合手工标量特征在跨项目泛化上匹配或超越更大架构。

Conclusion: 这是迈向SPICE引擎中紧凑、内容驱动的项目嵌入的第一步，应用于S2A3自适应测试系统。

Abstract: High-stakes computerized adaptive tests (CATs) must continually calibrate new items in their item bank. When an item is new, few responses are available, so item parameter estimates -- and thus test scores -- are poor. Item features and explanatory item response theory (IRT) models mitigate this by folding item content into calibration. Neural IRT models, whose item parameters are neural-net outputs, are powerful, but tuning hyperparameters and architectures in real time while a CAT is scoring is impractical and threatens validity. We propose a pre-launch step that fits a neural net to produce low-dimensional item embeddings, so the production system can use a simple linear explanatory IRT model on top of them. We use a neural parameterization of the 3-parameter logistic (3PL) model in which a feature network maps each item's content features to a representation $h_j = z(x_j) \in \mathbb{R}^d$, from which the discrimination and difficulty $(a,b)$ follow generalized linear forms; the guessing parameter $c$ is fixed to a global constant to avoid identifiability issues. The feature network and latent abilities $θ$ are fit jointly via Monte Carlo Expectation-Maximization (MCEM), with no separate ability-estimation or pre-calibration stage. Using an item-split protocol that holds out entire items to simulate feature-only evaluation, we apply this to two Duolingo English Test practice task types -- yes/no vocabulary and vocabulary-in-context -- searching over feature sets, architectures, and dimensions $d$. A shallow two-layer ReLU network with $d=6$ and hand-engineered scalar features matches or beats larger architectures on held-out items for both. This is a first step toward a compact, content-derived item embedding for the Scalable Parametric Item Calibration Engine (SPICE), the fully Bayesian engine at the core of the S2A3 adaptive-testing system.

</details>


### [117] [A Hierarchical Multilevel Inference Framework for Structural Cardiovascular Risk Modeling: County-Scale Analysis of Cardiovascular Mortality in Ohio and Pennsylvania (1999-2020)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06916)
*Christopher Blaszczak-Boxe,Jose Perez-Chavez,Sium Gebremeriam,King-Fai Li,Yuk L. Yung,Mohammad Russel*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 本文提出一个可重复的多水平统计推断框架，整合Normal、Poisson和人口偏移Poisson模型，用于量化嵌套地理单元内的心血管死亡率，并将人口统计效应与结构变异分离。应用于俄亥俄州和宾夕法尼亚州县级数据（1999-2020），发现不同模型揭示互补风险视角，单模型无法获取。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统流行病学分析常依赖聚合汇总或单一模型，无法揭示层级变异和背景异质性，心血管死亡率受多个空间尺度的人口、环境与结构过程交互影响，需要更精细的统计框架。

Method: 构建多水平统计推断框架，包含Normal（年龄调整）、Poisson（计数基础）和人口偏移Poisson三种模型；使用MLwiN建立层级模型，固定效应包括年份、性别、种族、PM2.5和O3，县级随机截距捕捉空间异质性；应用于俄亥俄州和宾夕法尼亚州县级心血管死亡率数据（1999-2020），覆盖七种CVD亚型。

Result: 年龄调整死亡率在宾夕法尼亚州下降快于俄亥俄州；Poisson模型识别出2010年后部分CVD亚型出现停滞或逆转；黑人群体死亡风险更高，男性死亡率高于女性；PM2.5在宾夕法尼亚州与缺血性和高血压性死亡率关联更强；人口偏移模型在保留县级结构差异的同时减少了未解释方差。

Conclusion: 该框架提供了单模型无法获得的互补风险视角，不仅适用于心血管流行病学，还是一个可推广至结构嵌套健康系统的层级统计方法，为疾病监测、环境健康评估、健康公平研究和可重复统计分析提供可扩展基础。

Abstract: Cardiovascular mortality is shaped by interacting demographic, environmental, and structural processes operating across multiple spatial scales. Conventional epidemiologic analyses often rely on aggregate summaries or single-model formulations that obscure hierarchical variation and contextual heterogeneity. We present a reproducible multilevel statistical inference framework integrating Normal (age-adjusted), Poisson (count-based), and population-offset Poisson models to quantify cardiovascular mortality across nested geographic units while separating demographic effects from structural variation.
  The framework was applied to county-level mortality data from Ohio and Pennsylvania (1999-2020) using MLwiN hierarchical models for seven cardiovascular disease (CVD) subtypes. Fixed effects included year, sex, race, PM2.5, and O3, while county-level random intercepts captured spatial heterogeneity. Complete model equations are provided in the Supplementary Material.
  The framework reveals complementary perspectives on cardiovascular risk unavailable from a single model. Age-adjusted mortality declined more rapidly in Pennsylvania than Ohio, whereas Poisson models identified post-2010 stagnation or reversal for several CVD subtypes. Black populations experienced elevated mortality risks, males exhibited higher mortality than females, and PM2.5 showed stronger associations with ischemic and hypertensive mortality in Pennsylvania. Population-offset models reduced unexplained variance while preserving county-level structural disparities.
  Beyond cardiovascular epidemiology, this work introduces a generalizable hierarchical statistical framework for structurally nested health systems. The methodology provides a scalable foundation for disease surveillance, environmental health assessment, health equity research, reproducible statistical analysis, and AI-assisted scientific inference.

</details>


### [118] [Multi-Trigger Crypto CAT Bonds with On-Chain Settlement: Valuation and Optimal Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06981)
*Yue Wang,Yijia Li,Maochao Xu,Xianyue Li*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对加密货币生态系统的双触发巨灾债券均衡定价框架，结合短期灾难冲击与长期系统性恶化的双触发结构，利用藤蔓Copula建模多风险依赖关系，并设计了链上结算架构以消除基差风险和代理成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 加密货币领域频繁遭受协议漏洞利用和交易所攻击导致大规模损失，使保险公司和投资者面临严重的操作风险，亟需有效的风险转移工具。

Method: 构建均衡定价框架，引入双触发结构（短期灾难冲击+长期系统性恶化），采用藤蔓Copula建模双重依赖关系（触发条件间依赖+金融风险因子间多元依赖），推导贴现现金流和回报率的完整分布，并设计基于智能合约的链上结算架构。

Result: 多触发加密CAT债券能够提供统计上稳健且经济高效的系统性数字资产风险转移工具，可计算VaR和TVaR等风险敏感指标。

Conclusion: 该框架通过链上结算消除传统中介的基差风险和代理成本，为将系统性数字资产风险有效转移至资本市场提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Cryptocurrencies have experienced repeated large-scale losses from protocol exploits and exchange breaches, exposing insurers and investors to severe operational risks. This paper develops an equilibrium pricing framework for catastrophe bonds tailored to the cryptocurrency ecosystem. We introduce a double-trigger structure that jointly captures short-term catastrophic shocks and longer-term systemic deterioration. To model the multi-risk environment, we incorporate dual dependence, combining dependence across triggers with multivariate dependence among financial risk factors through vine copulas. Beyond expected prices, we characterize the full distribution of discounted cash flows and return rates, enabling risk-sensitive metrics such as Value-at-Risk and Tail Value-at-Risk. Furthermore, we propose an on-chain settlement architecture where calibrated payout functions are embedded directly into smart contracts. This design eliminates basis risk associated with settlement delays and minimizes the agency costs inherent in traditional intermediation. Our results demonstrate that multi-trigger crypto CAT bonds offer a statistically robust and economically efficient vehicle for transferring systemic digital asset risks to capital markets.

</details>


### [119] [Which Regularized Propensity-Score and Doubly Robust Methods Are Best Calibrated When Exposures or Outcomes Are Rare? A Plasmode Study of Proxy-Based Confounding Adjustment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07065)
*M. Ehsan Karim,Wanqing Hu*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 本文通过plasmode模拟（基于NHANES数据，25个指定协变量+142个处方衍生代理变量）比较了十种结合变量选择策略（OAL、GLiDeR、HAL等）与IPTW/TMLE的因果推断管道，在已知零效应（RD=0）的常见、稀有暴露、稀有结局三种场景下评估偏倚、校准和计算成本。结果发现HAL（G-Computation）偏倚近乎零但估计集中导致覆盖近1且相对误差大（106-186%）；OAL-IPTW、GLiDeR和HAL-TMLE校准最佳；稀有暴露场景下LASSO-IPTW偏倚最大、SE膨胀、过度覆盖，TMLE可消除此问题。结论推荐结合结果感知选择（OAL、GLiDeR）或双重稳健估计（TMLE）以平衡偏倚、校准和对稀有性的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究缺乏在暴露患病率变化条件下，对多种正则化变量选择策略（OAL、GLiDeR、HAL）与双重稳健估计（TMLE）配对比较，且未在已知零效应基准下系统评估计算成本与校准表现。

Method: 采用plasmode模拟，基于NHANES数据（2013-2018年，25个 investigator-specified 协变量+142个处方衍生代理变量），固定真风险差RD=0，设计常见、稀有暴露、稀有结局三种场景，比较十种管道（OAL、GLiDeR、HAL等结合IPTW或TMLE），报告偏倚、标准误、相对误差、95%覆盖率和运行时间。

Result: HAL（G-Computation）偏倚近乎零但估计高度集中，覆盖近1、相对误差106-186%；OAL-IPTW、GLiDeR和HAL-TMLE校准最佳；正则化LASSO-TMLE在稀有场景下覆盖稍低（91-93%）；稀有暴露下LASSO-IPTW偏倚最大、SE膨胀、过度覆盖，TMLE可消除；真实数据各方法RD约0.07-0.085；运行时间从<1秒到>16小时不等。

Conclusion: 在零效应基准下，配对结果感知选择（OAL、GLiDeR）或双重稳健估计（TMLE）的正则化模型在偏倚、校准和对稀有性的鲁棒性上表现最佳；稀有暴露场景暴露了最大差距，方法选择应权衡优先指标与计算成本。

Abstract: Purpose. Confounding adjustment in health-care database studies screens large proxy libraries where events per variable are low, straining standard propensity score (PS) methods, and many regularized variable-selection strategies exist (outcome-adaptive LASSO [OAL], group LASSO/GLiDeR, highly adaptive LASSO [HAL]). Yet few comparisons have varied exposure prevalence within such a selection menu, and none pairs it with doubly robust estimation, compute accounting, and a null-RD truth anchor.
  Methods. We conducted a plasmode simulation anchored on National Health and Nutrition Examination Survey data (2013-2018; 25 investigator-specified covariates, 142 prescription-derived proxies), comparing ten pipelines combining these strategies with inverse probability of treatment weighting (IPTW) and targeted maximum likelihood estimation (TMLE). Three scenarios were evaluated under a known null (true risk difference, RD = 0): frequent, rare-exposure, and rare-outcome. We report bias, standard error (SE), relative error, 95% coverage, and runtime.
  Results. HAL (G-Computation) had near-zero bias but highly concentrated estimates, giving near-unity coverage and large relative error (106-186%). OAL (IPTW), GLiDeR, and HAL (TMLE) were best calibrated, whereas the regularized-LASSO TMLE pipelines under-covered modestly (91-93%) in the rare scenarios. Under rare exposure, LASSO-IPTW had the largest bias and inflated SE and over-covered (conservatively), problems that TMLE removed. On real data, methods agreed (RD approximately 0.07-0.085). Runtimes spanned <1 s to >16 h.
  Conclusions. Under a null benchmark, pairing outcome-aware selection (OAL, GLiDeR) or doubly robust estimation (TMLE) with regularized models best balanced bias, calibration, and robustness to rarity. The rare-exposure arm exposed the largest gaps; method choice should weigh the prioritized metric against compute.

</details>


### [120] [Nowcasting PM2.5 in Beijing Using Synchronous Covariates and Lagged Features: Model Comparison and Variable Selection Stability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07279)
*Yusheng Wang,Ruiyan Du*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 本文比较了正则化回归（Ridge、Lasso、Elastic Net）与深度学习模型（MLP、LSTM）用于北京12个监测站PM2.5同小时估算（现报）。MLP表现最佳（RMSE=13.651 μg/m³, R²=0.972），较线性模型RMSE降低约13%。滞后项和同步污染物是关键特征，Lasso倾向稀疏选择（4个稳定变量），Elastic Net保留相关变量组（11个稳定变量），CO、NO₂、PM10和一阶PM2.5滞后构成稳健核心集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PM2.5可靠现报对日常空气质量监测和城市管理具有实际重要性，而PM2.5浓度受排放源、气象条件、时间模式和站点异质性共同影响，解释变量间存在强相关性，需要系统比较不同建模方法在该任务上的表现。

Method: 使用2013年3月至2017年2月北京12个监测站逐小时数据，对比Ridge、Lasso、Elastic Net三种正则化回归与MLP、LSTM两种深度学习模型，采用时间戳顺序的时序训练/测试划分和TimeSeriesSplit交叉验证，并进行特征组分消融实验和变量选择稳定性分析（1SE规则）。

Result: MLP最优（RMSE=13.651, R²=0.972），较三种正则化回归（RMSE≈15.6, R²≈0.964）降低约13%误差；三种线性模型表现几乎一致；LSTM因CPU计算限制在子采样数据上表现不佳（RMSE=26.553, R²=0.889）。Lasso在1SE规则下选出4个稳定变量，Elastic Net选出11个；CO、NO₂、PM10和PM2.5一阶滞后构成稳健核心特征集。

Conclusion: MLP在PM2.5现报任务上优于线性模型；线性模型中正则化方法对相关性强的特征表现相近；滞后项和同步污染物是主要信息载体；变量选择策略影响模型可解释性与稳定性；模型误差存在空间和季节异质性。

Abstract: Reliable nowcasting of PM2.5 is of practical importance for daily air-quality monitoring and urban management. PM2.5 concentrations are jointly influenced by emission sources, meteorological conditions, temporal patterns, and station heterogeneity, and the explanatory variables exhibit strong correlations. This study compares regularized regression methods (Ridge, Lasso, Elastic Net) with deep learning models (MLP and LSTM) for same-hour PM2.5 estimation using hourly observations from 12 monitoring stations in Beijing from March 2013 to February 2017. Because the feature set includes both PM2.5 lagged terms and synchronous co-pollutant measurements, the task is framed as nowcasting rather than strict forecasting. Model evaluation employs timestamp-based chronological train/test splitting and TimeSeriesSplit cross-validation. The MLP achieves the best performance (RMSE = 13.651 ug/m3, R^2 = 0.972), reducing RMSE by approximately 13% relative to all regularized regression models (RMSE approximately 15.6, R^2 approximately 0.964). The three linear models perform nearly identically. As an exploratory supplement, the LSTM--constrained by CPU computational limitations to a subsampled training set--underperforms (RMSE = 26.553, R^2 = 0.889) and is not treated as a primary conclusion. Feature-group ablation shows that lagged terms and synchronous pollutants carry the dominant estimation information. Variable selection stability analysis under the 1SE rule reveals that Lasso favors sparse selection (4 stable variables) while Elastic Net retains correlated variable groups (11 stable variables); CO, NO2, PM10, and the first-order PM2.5 lag form a robust core set. Per-station and monthly error analyses further reveal spatial and seasonal heterogeneity in model errors.

</details>


<div id='math.OC'></div>

# math.OC [[Back]](#toc)

### [121] [A scalable linear programming-based framework for data clustering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06709)
*Aida Khajavirad,Huanwen Shen,Yakun Wang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文将De Rosa等人基于线性规划的K-means聚类算法扩展至公平K-means聚类和谱聚类两个重要领域。通过在线性不等式框架中融入群体公平约束和最小比率割问题的线性规划松弛，结合问题特定的初始化与舍入过程，实验表明该方法能在三小时内将90%的n≤3000实例求解至1%最优性范围内，且对56%以上的实例能找到优于流行启发式算法的解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将已有的基于线性规划的K-means聚类算法推广到公平K-means聚类和谱聚类这两个重要但更具挑战性的聚类范式，以弥补现有方法在可扩展性和解质量方面的不足。

Method: 1) 针对公平K-means聚类，将群体公平约束表示为线性数量的线性不等式，融入K-means聚类的划分矩阵形式中；2) 针对谱聚类，考虑最小比率割问题的线性规划松弛，使其适配同一框架；3) 为两种问题设计专门的初始化与舍入过程。

Result: 在大量真实数据集上，对于n≤3000的实例，90%可在三小时内达到1%最优性以内；超过56%的实例上，该算法找到的解优于流行的公平Lloyd型算法和谱聚类启发式算法。

Conclusion: 所提出的线性规划松弛在公平K-means和谱聚类两个应用场景中展现出显著优势，验证了该框架的强效性和通用性。

Abstract: We extend the linear programming-based algorithm of De Rosa et al~\cite{derKhaWan24} for K-means clustering to two important clustering paradigms: fair K-means clustering and spectral clustering. For fair K-means clustering, we show that widely used notions of group fairness can be incorporated into the partition-matrix formulation of K-means clustering through a linear number of linear inequalities. For spectral clustering, we consider a linear programming relaxation of the minimum ratio-cut problem that fits naturally within the same framework. We complement these formulations with problem-specific initialization and rounding procedures and evaluate the resulting algorithms on a large collection of real-world data sets. Denoting by $n$ the number of data points, our computational results demonstrate that the proposed approach solves $90\%$ of benchmark instances with $n \leq 3000$ to within $1\%$ optimality in at most three hours. This in turn demonstrates the remarkable strength of the proposed LP relaxations in both applications. Moreover, for more than $56\%$ of the instances, the proposed algorithm finds better solutions than those produced by popular fair Lloyd-type and spectral clustering heuristics.

</details>


### [122] [Optimization Geometrodynamics: A Framework for Dynamic Geometric Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06723)
*Zavier Li*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出优化几何动力学框架，将优化视为参数轨迹、粒子分布传输和时变黎曼度量的耦合演化，并定义了动态几何复杂度作为优化难度的度量基准。在强凸二次目标的全正定度量控制模型中，该复杂度等于相对对数谱到低条件数集合的仿射不变距离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有梯度优化方法通常使用固定的背景几何结构，忽略了参数内部状态隐式定义的可变长度、曲率和预条件。作者希望建立一种能分离不变障碍与可改进几何不匹配的基准语言，为可实现的适配优化器提供比较基准。

Method: 提出了优化几何动力学框架，将优化建模为参数轨迹、传输粒子分布和受控时变黎曼度量的耦合演化。定义了动态几何复杂度作为最小几何代价，并分析了Hessian匹配流、谱Onsager弛豫、离散指数投影更新、规范不变可观测量和固定时间局部Morse-鞍点通量。

Result: 证明了正度量保持临界点和Morse指标，无法消除全局测地凸性障碍，但可以改变条件数、分布传输和远离精确临界点的通量。在强凸二次目标的全正定度量控制模型中，动态几何复杂度等于相对对数谱到低条件数集合的仿射不变距离。

Conclusion: 本文为纯理论工作，提供的形式化断言和证明旨在为可实现的自适应优化器（在指定了允许度量族、曲率估计和离散化误差后）提供不变量和基准代价以供比较。

Abstract: Most gradient-based optimization methods move parameters through a fixed background geometry, even when their internal states implicitly define changing notions of length, curvature, and preconditioning. We introduce optimization geometrodynamics, a benchmark language in which optimization is a coupled evolution of a parameter trajectory, a transported distribution of particles, and a controlled time-varying Riemannian metric. The language separates invariant obstructions from improvable geometric mismatch: positive metrics preserve critical points and Morse indices, and cannot remove global geodesic-convexity obstructions, but can alter conditioning, distributional transport, and flux away from exact critical points. We introduce dynamic geometric complexity, the minimum geometric cost required to reduce an optimization difficulty observable. In the oracle benchmark model of strongly convex quadratic objectives with full positive-definite metric control, this complexity is exactly the affine-invariant distance from the relative log-spectrum to a low-condition-number set. We also analyze Hessian-matching flows, spectral Onsager relaxation, discrete exponential projection updates, gauge-invariant observables, and fixed-time local Morse-saddle flux. The paper is theory-only: its claims are formal statements with proofs, intended to provide invariants and benchmark costs against which implementable adaptive optimizers can be compared once their admissible metric families, curvature estimates, and discretization errors are specified.

</details>


### [123] [Linear-Quadratic Mean Field Games with Hybrid Local-Global Interactions on Manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06742)
*Tao Zhang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了紧致黎曼流形上具有混合交互拓扑的线性二次平均场博弈，构建了确定性局部图与随机非局部有向图叠加的网络结构。在无限总体极限下，系统由正倒向偏微分方程组控制。建立了Nash均衡的存在性，并利用算子集中不等式和解析半群理论导出了有限总体与连续极限之间的非渐近高概率误差界。稠密机制下误差呈依赖于流形维数和Sobolev正则性的多项式衰减，稀疏机制下为O((log N)^{-1/2})。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有平均场博弈研究多假设全局交互或简单图结构，缺乏对混合交互拓扑（局部几何连接+非局部随机交互）的理论分析。本文旨在建立此类复杂网络结构下的平均场极限理论，并量化有限总体逼近误差。

Method: 采用确定性图建模局部几何连通性，基于连续核K通过随机采样构造非局部有向图。利用算子集中不等式和解析半群理论分析误差，分别处理稀疏（出度Θ(log N)）和稠密（出度Θ(N)）两种拓扑机制。

Result: 证明了极限系统Nash均衡的存在性；稠密机制下跟踪误差呈依赖于流形维数和Sobolev正则性的多项式衰减，稀疏机制下误差以O((log N)^{-1/2})速率衰减。

Conclusion: 本文为混合交互拓扑的平均场博弈提供了严格的数学分析框架，揭示了网络稀疏性对逼近误差率的本质影响，为实际应用中的网络设计提供了理论指导。

Abstract: This paper studies linear-quadratic mean field games on compact Riemannian manifolds with a hybrid interaction topology. The network structure is a superposition of a deterministic graph for local geometric connectivity and a stochastic directed graph for non-local interactions. The global graph is constructed via random sampling based on a continuous kernel $K$. The out-degree of each node scales as $Θ(\log N)$ or as $Θ(N)$ to represent a sparse or dense network, respectively. In the infinite-population limit, the continuum system is governed by a coupled system of forward-backward partial differential equations, where the dynamics of the expected state incorporate the integral operator corresponding to the non-local sampling. The existence of a Nash equilibrium is established for this limit system. Furthermore, the approximation error is analyzed using operator concentration inequalities and analytic semigroup theory. Non-asymptotic high-probability error bounds between the finite-population empirical state and the continuum limit are derived. The convergence rates differ depending on the two topological regimes. Under the dense regime, the tracking error exhibits a polynomial decay rate dependent on the manifold dimension and Sobolev regularity, while under the sparse regime, the error decays at a rate of $\mathcal{O}((\log N)^{-1/2})$.

</details>


### [124] [Spectral Initialization and Certification for Power System Angle Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06762)
*Iven Guzel,Andrew D. McRae,Richard Y. Zhang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文证明，当电压幅值已知时，电力系统状态估计中的角度估计子问题可简化为相位同步问题，并揭示了谱方法成功的理论原因：在测量误差低于某个由经典可观测性裕度决定的阈值时，谱初始化可一阶精确恢复真实电压角度，且WLS估计量在全局相位意义下唯一；同时，零对偶间隙的谱证书可验证该唯一估计的恢复。数值实验支持理论并在保守保证范围外仍有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电力系统状态估计常被建模为非凸加权最小二乘问题，全局最优性难以实现和验证。近期工作表明，当电压幅值足够精确时，角度估计可简化为相位同步问题，并用谱方法有效求解。本文旨在从理论上解释这些谱方法为何成功。

Method: 论文通过理论分析证明谱初始化、谱证书的行为由测量误差与经典可观测性裕度的归一化比值决定。基于该阈值分析，推导了谱初始化恢复精度、WLS估计唯一性以及零对偶间隙证书的验证条件，并在无噪可观情形下给出精确恢复的结论。最后通过标准算例进行数值验证。

Result: 在低于固定阈值时，谱初始化可达一阶精度恢复真实电压角度，WLS估计量在全局相位意义下唯一，且零对偶间隙谱证书可验证该唯一估计的恢复。无噪可观情形下，谱初始化精确恢复真实角度，证书严格精确，无需局部精化。数值实验支持理论，且谱方法在保守保证范围外仍有效。

Conclusion: 本文为电力系统状态估计中谱方法的成功提供了严格的理论解释，揭示了测量误差与可观测性裕度之间的阈值关系，证明了谱初始化与谱证书的有效性，并通过实验展示了该方法超出理论保证范围的实用性。

Abstract: Power System State Estimation (PSSE) is commonly formulated as a nonconvex weighted least-squares (WLS) problem, making global optimality difficult both to attain and to certify. Recent work has shown that, when voltage magnitudes are known to sufficient accuracy, the remaining angle estimation subproblem can be reduced to phase synchronization and solved effectively using spectral initialization and spectral certification. This paper explains why these spectral methods succeed. We prove that their behavior is governed by the measurement error, normalized against the usual observability margin from the classical literature. Below a fixed threshold, spectral initialization recovers the true voltage angles to first-order accuracy, and the WLS estimator is unique up to a global phase. Moreover, a zero-duality-gap spectral certificate verifies recovery of this unique WLS estimate. In the noiseless observable regime, spectral initialization exactly recovers the true angles and certification is exact without local refinement. Numerical experiments on standard benchmark systems support the theory and show that the spectral methods remain effective beyond the conservative regime covered by the guarantees.

</details>


### [125] [Dynamic Driver Allocation Under Latent Demand Regimes: Indexability of a Partially Observed Markov Decision Process](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06816)
*Pedro Cesar Lopes Gerum,Jiong Liu,Ellen Bernal Cavalheiro,Luiz Felipe Martins,Matteo Giaretti*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对即时电商暗店（Quick-commerce dark stores）在15-30分钟内履约的订单配送问题，提出了一个基于部分可观测马尔可夫决策过程（POMDP）的配送人员动态调度模型。模型通过实时推断需求状态（regime）来优化司机配置，并证明了该问题的可索引性（indexability）。在此基础上，通过拉格朗日松弛将多店共享司机池问题分解为单店子问题，形成双层分配策略。基于欧洲公司SuperGlovo 2021-2022年数据的实证表明，信念更新（belief updating）相比固定信念可带来10.9%的收益提升（约18.8万美元/27家店），且收益集中在需求状态最持久的门店。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 即时电商（如Getir、Glovo、GoPuff）需在15-30分钟内完成履约，司机必须在订单到达前完成调度。需求存在跨小时持续的潜在状态（regime），未履约订单会累积为积压（backlog）。现有模型无法在订单到达时实时学习需求状态，导致调度效率低下。

Method: 将单店问题建模为POMDP，通过实时订单数据推断需求状态；证明单店问题具有可索引性（indexability），为多动作部分可观测问题提供可解性保障；通过拉格朗日松弛将共享司机池的网络问题分解为单店子问题，得到双层分配策略——定价需求实时追踪的价值，并按可验证的优先级指数对门店排序。

Result: 基于SuperGlovo 2021-2022年数据（西班牙《骑手法》下全职司机），信念更新相比固定信念带来10.9%的提升（约18.8万美元/27家店），收益集中在需求状态最持久（most persistent）的门店，且可在部署前观测到。在线分配简化为查表和按定价阈值排序的列表。

Conclusion: 提出并验证了一种能实时学习需求状态的即时电商司机调度方法，显著提升运营效益，且计算复杂度低、可部署性强。

Abstract: Quick-commerce dark stores dispatch e-grocery orders within 15 to 30 minutes, so operators such as Getir, Glovo, and GoPuff must commit drivers before orders arrive. Demand follows a latent regime that persists across hours, while unfulfilled orders spill forward as a compounding backlog. This decision binds whether drivers are employed on fixed shifts or drawn from a gig platform whose incentives are set ahead of the hour, yet existing models do not learn the regime as orders arrive. We formulate the single-store problem as a partially observable Markov decision process in which the firm infers the regime from realized orders. We show that optimal staffing rises with backlog and prove the single-store problem is indexable, a property open for multi-action partially observed problems in general. We then extend the framework to a driver pool shared across stores through a Lagrangian relaxation that decouples the network into per-store subproblems. The result is a two-level allocation policy that prices the value of tracking demand in real time and ranks stores by a provably valid priority index. On 2021 to 2022 data from the European firm SuperGlovo, which staffs full-time drivers to comply with Spain's Rider's Law, belief updating adds 10.9\%, about \$188K across 27 stores, over the same program with the belief held fixed. The gain concentrates in the stores whose regimes are most persistent, observable before deployment. Online allocation reduces to a table lookup and a ranked list with a cutoff price.

</details>


### [126] [Jacobi-like relative value iteration algorithms for ergodic risk-sensitive control of Markov chains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06863)
*Sumith Reddy Anugu,Guodong Pang,Nicola Sassone*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对遍历风险敏感控制问题，提出了Jacobi型和Gauss-Seidel型相对值迭代算法，在马尔可夫链不可约且常返的假设下证明了迭代的几何收敛性，并通过两个实例验证了算法有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 风险敏感控制的乘性代价结构导致Bellman算子缺乏全局收缩性和双Lipschitz连续性，使得传统平均代价设定下的收敛性证明方法失效，需要发展新的理论工具来保证迭代算法的收敛性。

Method: 提出了Jacobi-like相对值迭代(RVI)算法和Gauss-Seidel-like实现；建立了风险敏感Bellman算子的局部收缩性质及其不动点的局部双Lipschitz连续性，以此证明迭代的几何收敛速率。

Result: 在DTMC不可约且常返的假设下，证明了所提RVI算法的迭代序列以几何速率收敛；在单服务器队列服务努力控制和有限域退出率最大化两个实例上实现了算法。

Conclusion: 通过建立局部收缩和局部双Lipschitz连续性质，克服了风险敏感控制乘性结构带来的理论困难，为遍历风险敏感控制问题提供了收敛性有保证的RVI算法。

Abstract: We propose a Jacobi-like relative value iteration (RVI) algorithm and a Gauss-Seidel-like implementation for the ergodic risk-sensitive control (ERSC) problem of a controlled discrete time Markov chain (DTMC) on a finite state space. Under the assumption that the DTMC is irreducible and recurrent under every stationary Markov policy, we prove that the iterates of the proposed RVI algorithms converge at a geometric rate. The main challenge stems from the multiplicative structure of the ERSC cost criterion and the associated Bellman-like operators, which prevents us from adapting the analogous global contraction and bi-Lipschitz continuity properties that underlie the proof of convergence in the average cost setting. We overcome this by establishing local contraction properties for the risk-sensitive Bellman-like operators and a local bi-Lipschitz continuity property for their fixed points, and use these properties to show the iterates converge geometrically. We conclude by implementing our proposed RVI algorithms on two examples: service effort control for a single-server queue of finite capacity, and maximizing the exit rate from a finite domain (on a graph).

</details>


### [127] [Forgetting-Factor Regret for Online Zero-Sum Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07078)
*Yuhang Liu,Zi'ang Yan,Wenjun Mei,Wenxiao Zhao*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究在线两人零和博弈中的动态均衡跟踪问题，提出带遗忘因子的零和博弈遗憾函数，以指数衰减权重关注近期性能，实现时变纳什均衡的跟踪。针对一阶反馈（投影梯度下降-非对称上升和无投影Frank-Wolfe方法）和零阶反馈（确定性有限差分方法）三种算法，建立了遗忘因子遗憾界，并给出遗憾收敛到零的充分条件，数值实验验证了理论结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有在线鞍点问题的遗憾度量通常对历史收益赋予均匀权重，无法刻画对当前时变纳什均衡的实时跟踪性能。

Method: 提出带遗忘因子的零和博弈遗憾函数；分析三种算法：投影梯度下降-非对称上升、无投影Frank-Wolfe方法（一阶反馈），以及确定性有限差分方法（零阶反馈）。

Result: 建立了三种算法的遗忘因子遗憾界，显式刻画了NE变化、收益变化和梯度估计误差的影响；给出了遗憾收敛到零的充分条件，验证了时变NE的渐近跟踪能力。

Conclusion: 遗忘因子遗憾度量能有效实现时变纳什均衡的跟踪，数值实验支持理论结果。

Abstract: This paper studies dynamic equilibrium tracking in online two-player zero-sum games with time-varying convex-concave payoff functions. Existing regret metrics for online saddle-point problems usually aggregate historical payoffs with uniform weights, and hence may fail to characterize the real-time tracking performance with respect to the current Nash equilibrium (NE). To address this issue, we introduce a zero-sum game regret function with a forgetting factor, which assigns exponentially decaying weights to past saddle gaps and emphasizes recent performance. This metric directly links regret minimization to the tracking of time-varying NEs. Within this framework, we investigate three online algorithms under different computational and information settings. For first-order feedback, we analyze projected gradient descent-ascent and design a projection-free online Frank-Wolfe method to reduce the computational cost of projections. For zeroth-order feedback, we develop a deterministic finite-difference method that only uses function-value queries. For all three algorithms, we establish forgetting-factor regret bounds that explicitly characterize the effects of NE variation, payoff variation, and gradient-estimation error. We further provide sufficient conditions under which the proposed regret converges to zero, thereby certifying asymptotic tracking of time-varying NEs. The numerical example validates the theoretical results and illustrates the tracking advantage of the proposed regret metric.

</details>


### [128] [Model-Free Disturbance Observer with Online Modification: Listening to MFDOOM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07082)
*Nadav Barak,Christian Grussler*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出MFDOOM方法，通过基于先前预测误差的连续更新Hankel矩阵来修正输出预测，增强DeePC框架对扰动的鲁棒性，相比现有在线DeePC变体可降低跟踪误差和在线更新负担。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DeePC的性能依赖于所采集数据的相关性，扰动会导致不可避免的误差，现有方法在处理时变系统和扰动方面存在不足。

Method: 提出MFDOOM（Model-Free Disturbance Observer with Online Modification），利用基于先前预测误差的连续更新Hankel矩阵来修正DeePC的输出预测。

Result: 对于可建模为自治线性时不变系统输出的扰动，该方法相比现有在线DeePC变体可降低跟踪误差和在线更新负担。

Conclusion: MFDOOM增强方法能有效提升DeePC在扰动下的控制性能，尤其适用于特定类型扰动场景。

Abstract: Data-Enabled Predictive Control (DeePC) has recently emerged as a framework for controlling unknown systems from data. However, its performance relies on the relevance of the collected data, and as such, disturbances lead to inevitable errors. This paper addresses this problem by proposing an augmentation of DeePC using Model-Free Disturbance Observer with Online Modification (MFDOOM). The method corrects output predictions based on previous prediction errors using a dedicated continuously updated Hankel matrix. We compare our method, both theoretically and through simulation, to other recent algorithms designed for time-varying systems in the DeePC framework. It is shown that for disturbances that can be modeled as the output of an autonomous linear time-invariant system, this approach can reduce tracking error and online-update burden compared with existing online DeePC variants.

</details>


### [129] [Link-Based Multimodal Traffic Dynamics Model in Continuous-Time Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07152)
*Ning Xie,Lei Wei,Nikola Bešinović,Meng Wang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种多模式链路传输模型（M-LTM），该模型能够同时模拟连续的道路交通和离散的有轨电车交通在网络层面的交互。模型通过移动瓶颈理论捕捉模态间相互作用，并开发了节点模型模拟停靠站和交叉口的流传递、有轨电车运行及模式交互。案例验证显示该模型具有良好的预测能力，道路GEH统计量低于4，有轨电车到达时间绝对误差小于10秒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前文献缺乏能够有效模拟多模式交通流及其模态间交互的计算高效模型，这限制了协同交通管理的发展。

Method: 基于移动瓶颈理论开发多模式链路传输模型（M-LTM），包含链路模型和节点模型。节点模型分别处理有轨电车站点的停靠过程及引发的道路交通拥堵，以及交叉口的排队回溢和有轨电车优先通行。

Result: 在合成单节点网络、合成干道网络以及德累斯顿真实网络上进行案例研究，道路GEH统计量低于4，有轨电车平均到达时间绝对误差小于10秒。

Conclusion: M-LTM能够有效模拟多模式交通流及其交互，在网络交通分析和管理中具有良好的预测能力和适用性。

Abstract: Computationally efficient models for multimodal traffic flows with inter-modal interactions are foundational for coordinated traffic management. However, such models are lacking in the current literature. This study introduces a multimodal link transmission model (M-LTM) accommodating both continuous road traffic and discrete tramway traffic at the network level. M-LTM builds on a link model that captures the inter-modal interactions through the moving bottleneck theory. We further develop node models describing the flow transfer, tram operations, and mode interactions at tram stops and intersections. Specifically, the tram dwell process and dwell-induced congestion of road traffic are simulated at the stop node, and the intersection node model can reproduce queue spillback and tram priority. Case studies are conducted on two synthetic one-node networks, a synthetic arterial network, and the real network of Dresden to demonstrate the predictive power and the applicability of the proposed model in network traffic analysis and management. The GEH statistics of road traffic are below 4, and the average tram arrival time absolute errors are less than 10 seconds.

</details>


### [130] [Restricted Dynamic Geometric Complexity: Certificates for Structured Preconditioning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07204)
*Zavier Li*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文建立受限动态几何复杂度理论，将优化器状态视为演化几何结构，通过正定二次基准给出最小仿射不变形变以降低条件数，并发展了受限度量族下的内在证书距离、单调性、子流形距离原理、对角/分块可达性、精确二维对角复杂度公式、仿射不变Kronecker投影定理等核心结果，以及低秩谱模型、曲率代理膨胀、随机受限复杂度等诊断接口。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将结构预条件问题转化为几何距离、可达性和证书问题，建立从优化器状态几何视角统一分析的理论框架。

Method: 通过正定二次基准、受限动态几何复杂度、线性矩阵不等式可行性问题、Kronecker投影定理、Loewner-sandwich可达性条件等方法进行理论推导。

Result: 证明了单调性和子流形距离原理、对角和分块可达性、精确二维对角复杂度公式、仿射不变Kronecker投影定理及其伴随的Armijo求解器收敛性、自条件化K-target界、Hessian-相对候选证书等结果。

Conclusion: 本文提供了一个将结构预条件问题转化为几何距离、可达性和证书问题的完备语言框架，并附有确定性玩具和合成工作流验证。

Abstract: Optimization geometrodynamics views optimizer state as evolving geometry. Its full positive-definite quadratic benchmark gives the least affine-invariant deformation needed to reduce condition number when arbitrary metrics are allowed. This paper records that benchmark in the present notation and develops restricted dynamic geometric complexity: an intrinsic certificate distance for reaching a target condition-number class when the metric is restricted to a specified family. The main proved results are monotonicity and submanifold-distance principles, diagonal and block reachability as linear matrix inequality feasibility problems, an exact two-dimensional diagonal complexity formula, and affine-invariant Kronecker projection theorems with normal equations, computable mismatch certificates, Armijo solver convergence, auxiliary self-conditioned K-target bounds, and Hessian-relative candidate certificates through an exact Kronecker Loewner-sandwich reachability condition, including a Kronecker expression threshold and a fixed-basis exact subproblem. Low-rank spectral models, curvature-proxy inflation, stochastic restricted complexity, discrete geometric length, and expression--estimation--flow--discretization accounting are presented as diagnostic interfaces rather than full optimizer characterizations. The resulting language turns structural preconditioner questions into geometric distance, reachability, and certificate problems. The repository includes deterministic toy and synthetic workflows that check diagonal expression gaps, block primal/dual certificates, Kronecker spectral width, and Hessian-relative Kronecker candidate certificates on small quadratic instances, together with low-rank spectral monotonicity.

</details>


### [131] [POO-LPSP: Parallel Osprey Optimized Least Penalty-Squared Prioritization Methods for Priority Derivation in the Analytic Hierarchy Process](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07313)
*Kevin Kam Fung Yuen*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出并行鱼鹰优化最小惩罚平方排序法(POO-LPSP)，通过改进的鱼鹰优化算法高效求解AHP中的非线性优化模型，以最小化RMPSV和RMPSWV，提升排序可靠性，并在生成式AI供应商选择问题中验证了其实用性和计算效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统特征向量法在反映真实优先向量方面存在理论争议，且现有最小惩罚平方排序(LPSP)优化模型存在计算复杂度高、难以求解的问题。

Method: 提出并行鱼鹰优化最小惩罚平方排序法(POO-LPSP)，集成改进的并行鱼鹰优化算法(POOA)来高效求解修订后的LPSP非线性优化模型（包括LPPDS和LPPWS），以最小化RMPSV和RMPSWV。

Result: 通过生成式AI供应商选择问题的数值应用验证了POO-LPSP方法的实用性和计算效率，可作为Saaty特征系统法的鲁棒替代方案。

Conclusion: POO-LPSP能有效克服传统LPSP模型的计算复杂性，为AHP应用提供了一种更可靠的优先级排序方法，是特征向量法的强有力替代方案。

Abstract: Pairwise comparison (PC) via pairwise reciprocal matrices (PRMs) is central to the Analytic Hierarchy Process (AHP). Although the traditional eigenvector method is widely applied to derive priorities, its theoretical robustness in reflecting true priority vectors remains debated. Building upon a previous iteration of this study, this research develops the revised Least Penalty-Squared Prioritization (LPSP) optimization models, including the revised Least Product of Penalty and Direct Squares (LPPDS) and revised Weighted Squares (LPPWS), to minimize the revised Root Mean Penalty-Squared Variance (RMPSV) and the revised Root Mean Penalty-Weighted Square Variance (RMPSWV). However, solving these non-linear formulations is computationally complex for decision-makers. To overcome these limitations, this study proposes the Parallel Osprey Optimized Least Penalty-Squared Prioritization (POO-LPSP) method. By integrating an improved bio-inspired metaheuristic Parallel Osprey Optimization Algorithm (POOA), this framework efficiently solves complex LPSP models to minimize RMPSV and RMPSWV, thereby enhancing prioritization reliability. The practical utility and computational efficiency of the POO-LPSP method are validated through a numerical application focusing on a Generative AI (GAI) vendor selection problem. To extend, POO-LPSP can serve as a robust alternative to Saaty's Eigen system method for AHP applications.

</details>


### [132] [The linear regulator problem for passive systems with strong stability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07337)
*Bingsen Li,Hua-Cheng Zhou,George Weiss*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究阻抗无源线性系统的输出调节问题，采用经典谐振内模控制器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决阻抗无源线性系统的输出调节问题，利用谐振内模控制器实现系统稳定跟踪与扰动抑制。

Method: 基于经典谐振内模控制器，针对阻抗无源线性植物模型进行输出调节问题的分析与设计。

Result: 验证了谐振内模控制器在阻抗无源线性系统中的有效性与稳定性。

Conclusion: 谐振内模控制器可成功应用于阻抗无源线性系统的输出调节问题。

Abstract: We study the output regulator problem for an impedance passive linear plant, using the classical resonant internal model based controller.

</details>


### [133] [Maximal monotonicity of piecewise polyhedral mappings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07358)
*Xudong Li,R. Tyrrell Rockafellar,Defeng Sun*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究分段多面体结构下的极大单调映射，指出该结构允许绕过相对内部约束条件，从而在分裂方法中保持极大单调性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在分段线性-二次优化及分裂方法中，极大单调映射的分段多面体结构对于算法构造至关重要，但现有保持极大单调性的结论依赖于涉及某些相对内部非空性的约束规格，作者希望证明在分段多面体结构下可绕过这些约束。

Method: 利用分段多面体结构的特殊性，通过理论分析论证在该结构下不需要传统约束规格（相对内部非空性）也能保持极大单调性。

Result: 证明了分段多面体结构使得相对内部约束条件可以被绕过，即在无需传统约束规格的情况下仍可保持极大单调性。

Conclusion: 分段多面体结构为极大单调映射的算法构造提供了更宽松的适用条件，提升了分裂方法在优化中的效率与可行性。

Abstract: Maximally monotone mappings that are piecewise polyhedral arise from subdifferentials of convex functions and saddle functions that are piecewise linear-quadratic and enter into algorithmic constructions of importance in linear-quadratic optimization and associated splitting methods. The question of whether those constructions preserve maximal monotonicity is then crucial. The usual answers to that invoke constraint qualifications involving the nonemptiness of intersections of certain relative interiors, but it is shown here that the piecewise polyhedral structure allows the relative interiors to be bypassed.

</details>


### [134] [Improving greenhouse fruit-production control by integrating reinforcement learning into short-horizon model predictive control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07365)
*Bart van Laatum,Salim Msaad,Eldert J. van Henten,Robert D. McAllister,Sjoerd Boersma*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种轨迹选择RL-MPC框架，通过将强化学习的长期经济信息整合到短时域MPC优化中，在温室水果生产控制中实现了与高性能引导策略相当的闭环性能，同时显著优于相同预测时域的独立MPC。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 温室水果生产控制需要在外部天气扰动下最大化经济效益（果实收益减去运营成本），现有方法中，MPC能显式处理约束但长预测时域下计算负担大，RL能离线学习长期策略但难以保证约束且对未见天气轨迹泛化性差，因此需要一种结合两者优势的控制方法。

Method: 提出轨迹选择RL-MPC框架：(1) 使用RL的 rollout 轨迹定义终端区域约束和终端成本；(2) 非线性MPC在短时域内利用这些终端元件求解局部最优；(3) 从MPC预测轨迹和RL rollout轨迹中选择目标函数值更优的轨迹，执行其第一个输入。

Result: 在具有刚性强动力学的GreenLight大规模温室番茄生产模型上，采用1小时预测时域的轨迹选择RL-MPC达到了与高性能引导策略相当的闭环性能，且显著优于相同预测时域的独立MPC。

Conclusion: 轨迹选择RL-MPC有效结合了RL的长期经济性能优化能力和MPC的约束处理能力，在降低计算负担的同时保持了良好的控制性能，为温室生产控制提供了一种可行方案。

Abstract: Greenhouse fruit-production control aims to maximize the economic performance (fruit revenue minus operating costs) while operating within system constraints under external weather disturbances. Control methods need to balance the delayed economic benefit of fruit yield with current operating costs. For such problems, model predictive control (MPC) can explicitly handle system constraints under future weather disturbances, but can become computationally demanding when using sufficiently long prediction horizons for (relatively large) nonlinear greenhouse fruit production models. In contrast, reinforcement learning (RL) can learn control policies offline while considering longer-term economic performance, but struggles to enforce system constraints, and performance may degrade under unseen weather trajectories. This work proposes trajectory-selection RL-MPC, a framework that incorporates longer-term economic information of fruit yield into a short-horizon MPC optimization problem. The framework uses an RL rollout trajectory to define a terminal region constraint and terminal cost. Next, a nonlinear MPC solves a short-horizon optimization problem with these terminal ingredients to find a local optimum. Finally, the framework selects and executes the first input from the trajectory with the better objective value, either from the MPC-predicted or the RL rollout trajectory. The method is applied to GreenLight, a large-scale greenhouse tomato production model that exhibits stiff dynamics. The simulation results show that trajectory-selection RL-MPC with a one-hour prediction horizon matches the closed-loop performance of a high-performing guiding policy while significantly improving over standalone MPC with the same horizon.

</details>


### [135] [Tight Formulations for Unit Commitment with Different Levels of Details -- Part I: Models and Theoretical Insights](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07421)
*Maaike B. Elgersma,Karen I. Aardal,Mathijs M. de Weerdt,Germán Morales-España*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对机组组合（UC）问题中二进制变量导致的大规模计算瓶颈，提出了不同细节层次且基于凸包的紧致模型，并证明了启停、爬坡等经典公式的紧性，以在保证精度的同时降低计算负担。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: UC问题在大规模投资或随机模型中因二进制变量面临计算限制，现有方法要么降低模型精度（缩减规模），要么改进紧致性，但缺乏对不同发电机最佳公式的清晰概述，亟需系统化的紧致公式设计。

Method: 定义多个不同细节层次的模型，为每个层次提出基于凸包的公式；对启停成本/能力及爬坡约束的经典公式给出新的紧性证明。

Result: 获得了不同细节层次下基于凸包紧致的公式，这些公式可嵌入大规模问题中降低计算负担，并在Part II中验证了有效性。

Conclusion: 通过紧致公式与凸包理论，可在不牺牲模型保真度的前提下显著提升UC问题的计算性能，为大规模电力系统优化提供实用框架。

Abstract: The unit commitment (UC) problem is paramount for optimal operation of power systems, but it faces computational limitations in large-scale settings, especially in investment or stochastic models, because of the binary variables that it contains. A lot of research has attempted to improve the computational performance of UC models, either by reducing model size, resulting in lower fidelity and accuracy, or by improving the tightness of the formulation. Tightness and model size are the best a priori indicators of the computational performance of UC models, but there is no clear overview of what the best formulation is for different generators. In this research, we define models with different levels of detail, and present a formulation for each level that is based on the convex hull. We show new proofs on the tightness of well-known formulations for ramping, and for start-up and shut-down costs and capabilities. These models, with a different level of detail, can be incorporated into large-scale problems to reduce the computational burden, as demonstrated in Part II.

</details>


### [136] [How Much Spatial Control Is Enough? Subdomain Optimal Control of Reaction-Diffusion Systems in Synthetic Developmental Biology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07493)
*Mohamed Amine Ouchdiri,Hamza Faquir,Saad Benjelloun,Adnane Saoud*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了反应-扩散系统中控制区域受限时的最优控制问题，以Nodal-Lefty系统为例，推导了子域最优控制与全域最优控制之间损失的上界，给出了达到目标模式所需控制区域最小尺寸的显式公式，并证明了分散控制区域比单一控制区域更优的条件。数值实验表明覆盖约40%区域即可实现条纹到斑点的精确转换。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在实际应用中，控制只能作用于部分空间区域，但现有最优控制理论主要针对全域控制。本文旨在解决控制区域受限时的最优控制问题，量化子域控制与全域控制之间的性能差距。

Method: 以Nodal-Lefty反应-扩散系统为案例，将控制限制在子域上，推导了子域最优成本与全域最优成本之差（最优性损失）的显式上界；基于该上界，得到达到目标模式所需控制区域最小尺寸的显式公式；进一步分析了多个分散控制区域（总面积相同）与单一控制区域的对比。

Result: 获得了最优性损失的显式上界及控制区域最小尺寸公式；证明了在目标空间结构的自然条件下，分散控制区域比单一控制区域能获得更紧的上界；数值实验显示控制区域覆盖约40%的域即可高精度地将条纹模式转变为斑点模式。

Conclusion: 控制区域受限时仍可通过合理设计达到目标模式，分散控制策略在相同总面积下性能更优，为实际应用中的控制器布局提供了理论指导。

Abstract: Reaction-diffusion systems can produce spatial patterns such as stripes and spots through diffusion-driven instability. Steering these patterns from one configuration to another can be formulated as an optimal control problem. When the control acts on the entire spatial domain, existence and optimality conditions are well understood. Yet in practice, the control can only act on a part of the domain. Taking the Nodal--Lefty reaction--diffusion system as a case study, we consider the setting where the control is restricted to a subdomain. We derive an explicit upper bound on the optimality loss, defined as the difference between the subdomain optimal cost and the full-domain optimal cost. From this bound, we obtain an explicit formula for the minimum size of the control region needed to reach a target pattern with prescribed accuracy. We also consider the case where the control is distributed over several disjoint regions instead of a single one, with the same total area, and prove that the distributed configuration gives a tighter bound under natural conditions on the spatial structure of the target. Numerical illustrations confirm the theoretical results and show that a control region covering roughly forty percent of the domain is sufficient to drive the system from stripes to spots with high accuracy.

</details>


<div id='math.AT'></div>

# math.AT [[Back]](#toc)

### [137] [On $η$-periodic Formal Ternary Laws](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06795)
*Tao Huang*

Main category: math.AT

TL;DR: 本文研究η-周期动机稳定同伦范畴中Sp-定向的代数结构，发现Borel类确定的几何形式三元律通过HW-Hurewicz映射产生的万有系数环Λ是真子环，分类失败完全源于2-初级信息。通过引入框架对合得到分裂结构，并构造万有Walter环W^η，证明其在2可逆后与Lazard环同构，但整体上需要次级幂级数恢复完整定向信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解η-周期动机稳定同伦范畴中Sp-定向的完整代数结构，特别是解决Borel类分类不完整的问题，并探索2-初级信息缺失的本质。

Method: 引入框架对合（framed involution）概念，构造MSp[η^{-1}]上的典范框架对合，通过Quillen型幂等元得到分裂结构；公理化形式三元律并构造万有Walter环W^η，通过比较映射证明其与Lazard环的关系。

Result: (MSp[η^{-1}])_* ≅ R_fr ⊗_Z (MSL[η^{-1}])_*；W^η在2可逆后同构于Lazard环；分类映射φ: W^η→(MSp[η^{-1}])_*在2可逆后为同构，整体上为单射但非满射。

Conclusion: Sp-定向的代数分类在2-初级信息处存在根本性障碍，需要引入次级幂级数才能完整刻画，框架对合结构部分捕捉了缺失信息。

Abstract: We study the algebraic structure underlying Sp-orientations in the $η$-periodic motivic stable homotopy category $SH(k)[η^{-1}]$. Borel classes determine a geometric formal ternary law, but the HW-Hurewicz map shows that its universal coefficients generate a proper subring $Λ\subsetneq (MSp[η^{-1}])_*$, although $Λ[1/2]=(MSp[η^{-1}])_*[1/2]$. Thus the failure of classification is purely 2-primary. To capture part of the missing information, we introduce framed involutions. The spectrum $MSp[η^{-1}]$ carries a canonical framed involution, yielding a Quillen-type idempotent with telescope $MSL[η^{-1}]$ and a canonical splitting $(MSp[η^{-1}])_* \cong \mathcal{R}_{fr} \otimes_{\mathbb{Z}} (MSL[η^{-1}])_*$, where $\mathcal{R}_{fr}$ is the universal ring of framed involutions. We then axiomatize formal ternary laws, construct the universal Walter ring $\mathcal{W}^η$, and prove that $\mathcal{W}^η$ is isomorphic to the Lazard ring L after inverting 2. If $W(k)\cong \mathbb{Z}$, the universal geometric formal ternary law together with the canonical framed involution induces a classifying map $φ:\mathcal{W}^η\to (MSp[η^{-1}])_*$ that is injective and becomes an isomorphism after inverting 2. Integrally, however, additional secondary power series are needed to recover the full orientation data.

</details>


### [138] [Higher Semiadditive Character Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07431)
*Shaul Ragimov*

Main category: math.AT

TL;DR: 本文在高半可加性框架下引入了半可加特征理论，推广了T(n)-局部幺半特征和K(t)-局部跨色特征。该理论是与π-有限空间上的限制和转移映射兼容的自然变换，在目标中嵌入了(n-t)重p-典型自由环路空间修正。证明了每个∞-交换幺半群都存在一个普适的(n-t)重特征，该特征具有蓝移、高环游轮下降以及与半可加傅里叶变换兼容等结构性质，并计算了其在K(n)-局部对象上的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在高等半可加性设定下统一并推广已有的T(n)-局部幺半特征和K(t)-局部跨色特征理论，建立更一般的半可加特征框架。

Method: 发展了高半可加性设定下的半可加特征理论，构造了与π-有限空间上限制和转移映射兼容的自然变换，在目标中引入(n-t)重p-典型自由环路空间修正，并证明了普适特征的存在性及其结构性质。

Result: 证明了每个∞-交换幺半群存在普适的(n-t)重特征，该特征具有蓝移、高环游轮下降和与半可加傅里叶变换兼容的性质；计算了任意K(n)-局部对象上的特征，表明对Morava E-理论恢复为K(t)-局部跨色特征；证明了普适特征带有GL_{n-t}(Z_p)作用，t=0时不动点恢复为有理化；导出了L_Q(S^A_{K(n)})的显式描述并计算了有理K(n)-局部幂运算环。

Conclusion: 建立了统一的高半可加特征理论框架，揭示了普适特征的蓝移、下降和与傅里叶变换兼容等深层结构性质，并通过GL_{n-t}(Z_p)作用与有理化的联系给出了具体的计算应用。

Abstract: We introduce and develop the theory of semiadditive characters in the higher semiadditive setting, generalizing both the $T(n)$-local monoidal character and the $K(t)$-local transchromatic character. These are natural transformations compatible with restriction and transfer maps along $π$-finite spaces, with an $(n-t)$-fold $p$-typical free loop space correction built into the target. We show that every $\infty$-commutative monoid admits a universal $(n-t)$-fold character. This universal character has several strong structural properties: it exhibits blue shift, satisfies higher cyclotomic descent, and is compatible with the semiadditive Fourier transform. We compute it for an arbitrary $K(n)$-local object and show that, for Morava $E$-theory, it recovers the $K(t)$-local transchromatic character. By functoriality, the universal character carries a natural action of the profinite group $\mathrm{GL}_{n-t}(\mathbb{Z}_p)$. When $t=0$, the fixed points of this action recover rationalization. As a consequence, we derive an explicit description of $L_{\mathbb{Q}}(S^A_{K(n)})$ for every $π$-finite space $A$ and compute the ring of rational $K(n)$-local power operations.

</details>


<div id='math.RT'></div>

# math.RT [[Back]](#toc)

### [139] [A field-independent filtration of plethystic modules for $\mathrm{SL}_2(\mathbb{F})$ that categorifies a product rule for the Cartan subalgebra of $\mathcal{U}_q(\mathfrak{sl}_2)$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06749)
*Álvaro Gutiérrez,Álvaro L. Martínez,Michał Szwej,Mark Wildon*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 作者将量子sl₂的Cartan子代数中的乘积法则提升为SL₂的Weyl函子Δ^(n,m)作用在对称幂Sym^d E上的一个滤过结构，这是量子sl₂范畴化的重要一步，也是首个需要多层滤过的域无关同构结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推进量子sl₂的范畴化，并扩展SL₂表示论中一系列域无关同构（如Hermite互反律、Wronskian同构）的家族，填补需要多层滤过结构的空白。

Method: 利用作者此前建立的Weyl模的对称函数模型，通过组合技巧将量子sl₂的Cartan子代数中的乘积法则提升到plethystic表示Δ^(n,m) Sym^d E的滤过上。

Result: 得到了Δ^(n,m) Sym^d E的一个滤过结构，该结构是域无关的，并且是首个需要多个滤过层的此类同构结果。

Conclusion: 该滤过结构是量子sl₂范畴化道路上的重要进展，并丰富了SL₂表示论中域无关同构的成果体系。

Abstract: We lift a product rule in the Cartan subalgebra of quantum $\mathfrak{sl}_2$ to a filtration of the plethystic representation $Δ^{(n,m)}\mathrm{Sym}^d E$ of the affine group scheme of the algebraic group $\mathrm{SL}_2$, where $E$ is the natural representation and $Δ^{(n,m)}$ the Weyl functor. This is a significant step towards a categorification of quantum $\mathfrak{sl}_2$. Our filtration is an addition to a growing family of field-independent isomorphisms of $\mathrm{SL}_2$ representations that include Hermite reciprocity and the Wronskian isomorphism. It is the first such field-independent result requiring multiple filtration layers. It is proved by combinatorial techniques using the authors' symmetric functions model for Weyl modules.

</details>


### [140] [Covers of Bruhat-Tits trees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06899)
*Corina Ciobotaru,Peter Vang Uttenthal*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文研究局部紧群$G$在齐性空间$G/K$上的自然作用如何通过显式上闭链提升到中心扩张$\widetilde{G}$，并应用于非Archimedes局部域上构造$\operatorname{GL}_2(F)$的metaplectic覆盖作用的连通局部有限树，证明Gelfand对性质，以及从树几何角度描述抛物子群和收缩子群。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为metaplectic覆盖群（特别是$\operatorname{GL}_2(F)$的metaplectic覆盖）建立类似于Bruhat-Tits树的几何实现，以便利用几何工具研究其表示论和群结构。

Method: 通过构造一个显式上闭链，将$G$在$G/K$上的自然作用提升到中心扩张$\widetilde{G}$；利用非Archimedes局部域上的Bruhat-Tits理论，构造一个连通局部有限树，使metaplectic覆盖群作用在该树上；通过分析作用性质证明Gelfand对；从树几何角度刻画抛物子群与收缩子群。

Result: (1) 获得了将$G/K$上的作用提升到$\widetilde{G}$的显式上闭链；(2) 构造了$\operatorname{GL}_2(F)$的metaplectic覆盖作用的一个连通局部有限树，这是Bruhat-Tits树的几何类比；(3) 在合适的传递性假设下证明了$(\widetilde{G},\widetilde{K})$是Gelfand对；(4) 从树几何角度描述了$\widetilde{G}$的抛物子群和收缩子群。

Conclusion: 该工作为一般局部紧群的中心扩张提供了几何实现方法，特别是为metaplectic覆盖群建立了类似Bruhat-Tits树的几何框架，有望推动相关表示论和调和分析的研究。

Abstract: Let $G$ be a locally compact group and let $\widetilde{G}$ be a central extension that splits over a maximal compact subgroup $K$ of $G$. We derive an explicit cocycle that lifts the natural action of $G$ on the homogeneous space $G/K$ to an action of $\widetilde{G}$. As an application, for a non-Archimedean local field $F$, we construct a connected locally finite tree on which the metaplectic covers of $\operatorname{GL}_2(F)$ act by automorphisms, providing a geometric analog of the Bruhat--Tits tree of $\operatorname{GL}_2(F)$. Furthermore, under suitable transitivity assumptions, we prove that $(\widetilde{G},\widetilde{K})$ is a Gelfand pair. Finally, we describe the associated parabolic and contraction subgroups with respect to $\widetilde{G}$ from the perspective of the geometry of the constructed tree.

</details>


### [141] [Harish-Chandra images of orthosymplectic Sugawara operators and Casimir elements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06926)
*Alexander Molev,Madeline Nurcombe,Eric Ragoucy*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文研究了正交辛李超代数的Segal-Sugawara向量，计算了其在Harish-Chandra同态下的像，并推广到相关的Sugawara算子和Casimir元素；同时还构造了高阶Gaudin Hamiltonians和量子参数平移子代数中的元素。附录回顾了一般线性李超代数的类似结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩展Segal-Sugawara构造从一般线性李超代数到正交辛李超代数，并建立其与Harish-Chandra同态、Casimir元素及可积系统（Gaudin模型）的联系。

Method: 利用Harish-Chandra同态计算Segal-Sugawara向量的像，并将其推广到Sugawara算子和Casimir元素；通过此构造得到高阶Gaudin Hamiltonians和量子shift-of-argument子代数元素。

Result: 成功计算了正交辛李超代数Segal-Sugawara向量在Harish-Chandra同态下的像，给出了对应的Sugawara算子和Casimir元素，并构造了高阶Gaudin Hamiltonians及量子shift-of-argument子代数元素。

Conclusion: 将Segal-Sugawara构造从一般线性超代数情形推广到正交辛情形，建立了与Harish-Chandra同态和可积系统的联系，附录中回顾了一般线性情形的平行结果作为对照。

Abstract: We consider the recently constructed Segal--Sugawara vectors for the orthosymplectic Lie superalgebras. We calculate their images with respect to the Harish-Chandra homomorphism and extend this calculation to the associated Sugawara operators and Casimir elements. We also produce higher Gaudin Hamiltonians and elements of the quantum shift-of-argument subalgebras in the orthosymplectic enveloping algebra. In the Appendix, we review analogous results for the general linear Lie superalgebras.

</details>


### [142] [A categorification of the Brenti--Welker identity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06927)
*Deke Zhao,Zhankui Xiao*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文对Brenti-Welker恒等式进行了范畴化，将其从计数等式提升为对称群表示的同构，通过研究(ℂ^r)^⊗n与Foulkes特征模的张量积分解来实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将Brenti-Welker关于Eulerian数的恒等式从枚举等式提升为对称群表示的同构，即进行范畴化。

Method: 研究(ℂ^r)^⊗n与承载Foulkes特征的模作为对称群模的张量积分解，利用一个可能具有独立意义的组合恒等式作为证明的主要工具。

Result: 建立了Eulerian数的Brenti-Welker恒等式的范畴化版本，即对称群表示的同构关系。

Conclusion: 成功将Brenti-Welker恒等式从计数层面提升至表示论层面，揭示了其深层结构。

Abstract: The paper aims to provide a categorification of the Brenti--Welker identity involving Eulerian numbers in (Adv. Appl. Math. 42 (2009): 545--556) by lifting it from an enumerative equality to an isomorphism of symmetric group representations. To do so, we study the decomposition of the tensor product of $(\mathbb{C}^r)^{\otimes n}$ and modules affording Foulkes characters as modules of the symmetric group. The main ingredient of the proof is a combinatorial identity which may be of independent interest.

</details>


### [143] [From $(n+1)$-term subcategories to $(n+1)$-term complexes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06960)
*Zhaotai Zhang,Yu Zhou,Bin Zhu*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文从代数三角化范畴中的n-刚性子范畴出发，构造了一个到有界同伦范畴中(n+1)-项复形的extriangulated函子，并在约化(n-1)-Auslander extriangulated范畴的框架下给出了该函子为满的充要条件，进而建立了这些范畴之间silting子范畴的突变兼容双射，最后将结果应用于(n+1)-Calabi-Yau范畴中的n-簇倾斜子范畴和对象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立从代数三角化范畴中由n-刚性子范畴生成的(n+1)-项子范畴到有界同伦范畴中(n+1)-项复形全子范畴之间的extriangulated函子，并在约化(n-1)-Auslander extriangulated范畴的框架下研究该函子的满性条件，进而建立silting子范畴之间的对应关系。

Method: 通过显式构造extriangulated函子，利用约化(n-1)-Auslander extriangulated范畴的理论框架，分析函子为满的充要条件，并建立模某个理想的extriangulated范畴等价，以及silting子范畴之间的突变兼容双射。

Result: 成功构造了所需的extriangulated函子，给出了该函子为满的充要条件（此时诱导模某个理想的extriangulated范畴等价），建立了silting子范畴之间的突变兼容双射，并将结果应用于(n+1)-Calabi-Yau范畴中的n-簇倾斜子范畴和对象。

Conclusion: 本文在extriangulated范畴的框架下统一并推广了相关结构，为n-刚性子范畴和n-簇倾斜理论提供了新的函子构造和等价关系。

Abstract: Let $n$ be a positive integer. Given an $n$-rigid subcategory $\mathcal{M}$ of an algebraic triangulated category $\mathcal{T}$, we explicitly construct an extriangulated functor from the $(n+1)$-term subcategory of $\mathcal{T}$ generated by $\mathcal{M}$ to the full subcategory of $(n+1)$-term complexes in the bounded homotopy category $K^b(\mathcal{M})$, which restricts to the identity on $\mathcal{M}$. In the broader context of reduced $(n-1)$-Auslander extriangulated categories, we provide necessary and sufficient conditions for such a functor to be full, in which case it induces an equivalence of extriangulated categories modulo a certain ideal. Furthermore, we establish a mutation-compatible bijection between the silting subcategories of these categories. Finally, we apply these results to $n$-cluster tilting subcategories and $n$-cluster tilting objects in $(n+1)$-Calabi-Yau categories.

</details>


### [144] [Extriangulated ideal quotients and $d$-Auslander categories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07211)
*Lior Silberberg*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文建立了$d$-Auslander 扩张三角范畴与$(d+2)$-项复形同伦范畴之间的联系，给出了代数扩张三角范畴具有同构于$\mathcal{K}^{[-d-1,0]}(\mathcal{A})$的扩张三角理想商的条件，证明了三角范畴中的$d$-簇倾斜子范畴是$d$-Auslander 扩张三角范畴的关键来源，并回答了Iyama在附录中提出的问题：当$\mathcal{N}$是弱幂等完备代数$(d+4)$-角范畴时，$\mathcal{K}^{[-d-1,0]}(\mathcal{N})$具有三角结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立$d$-Auslander 扩张三角范畴与$(d+2)$-项复形同伦范畴之间的深层联系，并回答Iyama在论文附录中提出的具体问题。

Method: 通过研究$d$-Auslander 范畴的已有成果，建立代数扩张三角范畴的扩张三角理想商与复形同伦范畴同构的精确同调条件，并利用三角范畴中$d$-簇倾斜子范畴作为$d$-Auslander 扩张三角范畴的关键来源。

Result: 给出了代数扩张三角范畴的扩张三角理想商等价于$\mathcal{K}^{[-d-1,0]}(\mathcal{A})$的同调条件；证明了$d$-簇倾斜子范畴是$d$-Auslander 扩张三角范畴的关键来源；证明了当$\mathcal{N}$是弱幂等完备代数$(d+4)$-角范畴时，$\mathcal{K}^{[-d-1,0]}(\mathcal{N})$具有三角结构。

Conclusion: 成功建立了$d$-Auslander 扩张三角范畴与复形范畴之间的联系，并回答了Iyama提出的公开问题。

Abstract: Building on recent studies of 0-Auslander categories, we establish a connection between $d$-Auslander extriangulated categories and categories of $(d+2)$-term complexes up to homotopy. We give a precise homological condition under which an algebraic extriangulated category admits an extriangulated ideal quotient equivalent to $\mathcal{K}^{[-d-1,0]}(\mathcal{A})$. We then demonstrate that $d$-cluster-tilting subcategories in triangulated categories serve as a key source of $d$-Auslander extriangulated categories. Using these structural results, we answer a question posed by Iyama in the Appendix of arXiv:2509.08246 by proving that $\mathcal{K}^{[-d-1,0]}(\mathcal{N})$ admits a triangulated structure when $\mathcal{N}$ is a weakly idempotent complete algebraic $(d+4)$-angulated category.

</details>


### [145] [Aubert duality and co-tempered data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07512)
*Nans Bonnel*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 针对非阿基米德局部域F（特征0）上的辛群Sp_{2n}(F)和特殊正交群SO_{2n+1}(F)，提出一种新的算法来计算Langlands数据层面的Aubert对偶。该算法作为Lanard-Mínguez算法的对偶，采用自底向上的方法并在内部计算中严格保持表示的缓变性，从而自然得到共缓变表示的新构造性刻画。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Lanard-Mínguez算法采用自顶向下的方法计算Aubert对偶，需要一个新算法从自底向上、保持缓变性的角度补充和推进这一研究方向，特别是为了给出共缓变表示的构造性刻画。

Method: 提出一种自底向上的新算法，其内部计算严格保持表示的缓变性（temperedness），在Langlands数据层面计算Aubert对偶。

Result: 该算法自然得到共缓变表示的新构造性刻画；给出了共缓变表示的缓变分量精确描述；建立了一大类缓变表示的显式对偶公式。

Conclusion: 通过仅在缓变数据范围内操作，该算法支持对共缓变表示的性质进行归纳证明，为理解Aubert对偶与共缓变表示提供新工具。

Abstract: Let $F$ be a non-archimedean local field of characteristic 0, and let $G$ be either $\Sp_{2n}(F)$ or $\SO_{2n+1}(F)$. We introduce a new algorithm to compute the Aubert dual at the level of Langlands data. This algorithm acts as the dual to the recent Lanard-Mínguez algorithm. It fundamentally differs in two ways: it follows a bottom-up approach rather than a top-down one, and its internal computations strictly preserve the temperedness of the representations. Consequently, this approach naturally yields a new constructive characterization of co-tempered representations.
  By operating exclusively within the realm of tempered data, this algorithm enables inductive proofs of new properties for co-tempered representations. In particular, we provide a precise description of their tempered components and establish an explicit duality formula for a large class of tempered representations.

</details>


### [146] [Geometry and topology of the tempered Iwahori-spherical representations of a split semisimple $p$-adic group](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07525)
*Dominic Majda,Graham A. Niblo,Roger Plymen,Nick Wright*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文为类型 $B_n, C_n, D_n$ 的连通分裂 $p$-adic 群的 Iwahori-球面块提供了紧 Hausdorff 模型，并通过扩展商（由扇区分层）计算了对应 $C^*$-代数的 $K$-理论秩。几何描述了每个扇区，发现了实射影空间及其锥和悬垂结构，并修正了同伦扇区猜想，证明了 Langlands 对偶扇区在有理（dyadic）同伦等价意义下对所有类型成立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在 $p$-adic 群的表示理论中，Iwahori-球面块对 tempered 表示起关键作用。为了理解其 $C^*$-代数的 $K$-理论秩，需要构造一个良好的拓扑模型。同时，ABPS 猜想框架下的几何扩展商与谱扩展商之间的相互作用需要进一步澄清，特别是对于 $B_n, C_n, D_n$ 等类型。

Method: 构造了一个紧 Hausdorff 模型（扩展商），该模型由扇区分层；利用几何与谱扩展商之间的对偶关系，对每个扇区给出几何描述，赋予谱以细胞结构；分类模型中出现的几何结构（实射影空间、锥、悬垂）；修改同伦扇区猜想，证明 Langlands 对偶扇区在有理（dyadic）同伦等价意义下成立。

Result: 成功计算了 $C^*$-代数的 $K$-理论秩；给出了每个扇区的几何描述；发现了实射影空间以及它们的锥和悬垂等几何结构；证明了对于所有 $A_n$ 到 $E_8$ 类型，Langlands 对偶扇区是 dyadic 同伦等价的。

Conclusion: 该模型统一处理了 $B_n, C_n, D_n$ 类型的 Iwahori-球面块，揭示了 Langlands 对偶扇区在有理同伦意义下的等价性，为 ABPS 猜想提供了新的证据，并修正了之前关于同伦扇区猜想的理解。

Abstract: Let $\mathfrak{G}$ be a connected split $p$-adic group of type $B_n$, $C_n$ or $D_n$. Amongst the tempered representations of $\mathfrak{G}$ a key role is played by the Iwahori-spherical block. We provide a compact Hausdorff model for this space which allows us to compute the $K$-theory ranks for the corresponding $C^*$-algebra. The model, an extended quotient, is stratified by sectors. Underlying the approach of this paper is the interplay between the geometric extended quotient and the spectral extended quotient in the context of the ABPS conjecture.
  We give geometric descriptions of every sector thus equipping the spectrum with a cellular structure. We classify the geometric structures arising in our model, and specifically discover real projective spaces along with cones and suspensions of these. These examples require a minor modification to our homotopy sector conjecture: we show that Langlands dual sectors are rationally (indeed dyadically) homotopy equivalent in all cases ($A_n, B_n, C_n, D_n, E_6, E_7$ and $E_8$).

</details>


### [147] [Magnitude of module categories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07555)
*Erlend D. Børve,Daniel Horiatakis,Martin Kalck*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文定义了表示有限代数的模范畴的不变量——Auslander代数的magnitude，并称之为模范畴的magnitude。对于有界路代数，可通过Auslander-Reiten箭图的欧拉示性数计算。作者通过定义平移箭图的Auslander-Reiten-Euler示性数，计算了双列代数、遗传路代数、平方零根有界路代数和自内有界路代数等情形下的magnitude，并将其表达为Grothendieck群的秩和Dynkin箭图的Coxeter数等已知量。基于计算结果，提出了关于表示有限双列代数的一个猜想性刻画。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为表示有限代数的模范畴定义一个新的不变量，并建立其与已知代数不变量（如Grothendieck群的秩、Coxeter数等）之间的联系，从而为代数分类提供新的工具。

Method: 通过定义Auslander代数的magnitude作为模范畴的不变量；对平移箭图定义Auslander-Reiten-Euler示性数，用于计算有界路代数情形的magnitude；运用Auslander-Reiten理论中的经典结果进行具体计算。

Result: 计算了双列代数、遗传路代数、平方零根有界路代数和自内有界路代数等几类表示有限代数的模范畴的magnitude，将其表达为Grothendieck群的秩和Dynkin箭图的Coxeter数等已知量。

Conclusion: 基于计算结果，提出了一个关于表示有限双列代数在模范畴magnitude和Grothendieck群秩意义下的猜想性刻画。

Abstract: We define an invariant of the module category of a representation-finite algebra by the magnitude of its Auslander algebra. This invariant will be called the magnitude of the module category. For bound path algebras, it can be computed as the Euler characteristic of the Auslander--Reiten quiver, in a suitable sense. To aid the computation of our invariant, we define the Auslander--Reiten--Euler characteristic of a translation quiver. We build on classical results in Auslander--Reiten theory to determine the magnitude of module categories of biserial algebras, hereditary path algebras, radical square zero bound path algebras, and self-injective bound path algebras. In these cases, we express our invariant in terms of other known quantities, notably the rank of the Grothendieck group and Coxeter numbers of Dynkin quivers. Based on our calculations and results, we obtain a conjectural characterisation of representation-finite biserial algebras in terms of the magnitude of the module category and the rank of the Grothendieck group.

</details>


### [148] [On Two Algebraic Realizations of Schubert Calculus](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07667)
*André Contiero,Letterio Gatto,Parham Salehyan*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文系统发展了复Grassmannian上Schubert计算的两种形式：基于Schur多项式微分算子的玻色子形式和基于外代数顶角算子的费米子形式，并通过玻色-费米对应建立了统一框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将Schubert calculus与Schur多项式、外代数、对称群表示论等领域的工具统一起来，建立系统的形式化框架。

Method: 分别发展玻色子形式（Schur多项式上的微分算子）和费米子形式（外代数上的顶角算子），并通过有限型玻色-费米对应将二者联系起来。

Result: 建立了连接Schubert calculus、Grassmannian积分、对称函数、外代数和对称群表示论的统一框架。

Conclusion: 玻色-费米对应为Schubert calculus提供了统一的理论视角，将多个数学分支自然地融合在一起。

Abstract: Schubert calculus on complex Grassmannians can be played by means of differential operators acting on Schur polynomials or Vertex Operators acting on exterior algebras. In this paper we develop this point of view systematically and complement it with a parallel exterior-algebra formalism, leading to what we call, respectively, the \emph{bosonic} and the \emph{fermionic} Schubert calculus. The two alluded realizations are related by (a finite type version of) the boson--fermion correspondence, thereby providing a unified framework connecting Schubert calculus and integrals on the Grassmannian, symmetric functions, exterior algebras and the representation theory of symmetric groups.

</details>
